def default_pipeline(category_key: str) -> dict[str, Any]:
    return deepcopy(DEFAULT_PIPELINES.get(category_key, DEFAULT_PIPELINES["space_image"]))


def legacy_preprocess_to_pipeline(category_key: str, raw: Mapping[str, Any] | None) -> dict[str, Any]:
    config = dict(raw or {})
    pipeline = default_pipeline(category_key)
    if config.get("preprocess") == "pdf":
        pipeline = default_pipeline("pdf_text")
        pipeline["processors"][0]["config"] = {
            "max_pages": config.get("max_pages", 4),
            "max_text_chars": config.get("max_text_chars", 24000),
        }
    if config.get("material_focus") is True:
        pipeline = default_pipeline("material_image")
    elif category_key == "material_image" and config.get("material_focus") is False:
        pipeline = default_pipeline("material_image")
        pipeline["processors"][-1]["config"]["enabled"] = False
    return pipeline


def _validate_config(module: str, config: Any) -> dict[str, Any]:
    if not isinstance(config, dict):
        raise ValueError(f"处理模块 {module} 的参数必须是对象")
    schema = PROCESSOR_CATALOG[module].get("config_schema", {})
    unknown = set(config) - set(schema)
    if unknown:
        raise ValueError(f"处理模块 {module} 包含未知参数：{', '.join(sorted(unknown))}")
    normalized: dict[str, Any] = {}
    for key, rule in schema.items():
        value = config.get(key, rule.get("default"))
        if value is None:
            continue
        if rule["type"] == "integer" and (isinstance(value, bool) or not isinstance(value, int)):
            raise ValueError(f"处理模块 {module}.{key} 必须是整数")
        if rule["type"] == "boolean" and not isinstance(value, bool):
            raise ValueError(f"处理模块 {module}.{key} 必须是布尔值")
        if rule["type"] == "integer" and not rule["min"] <= value <= rule["max"]:
            raise ValueError(f"处理模块 {module}.{key} 超出允许范围")
        normalized[key] = value
    return normalized


def dimension_options_from_definition(
    definition: Mapping[str, Any] | None,
) -> list[dict[str, Any]]:
    """Return stable frontend metadata without mutating a schema definition."""

    if not isinstance(definition, Mapping):
        return []
    dimensions = definition.get("dimensions")
    if not isinstance(dimensions, list):
        return []
    options: list[dict[str, Any]] = []
    seen: set[str] = set()
    for index, item in enumerate(dimensions, start=1):
        if not isinstance(item, Mapping):
            return []
        key = item.get("key")
        label = item.get("label")
        if (
            not isinstance(key, str)
            or not key
            or key in seen
            or not isinstance(label, str)
            or not label
        ):
            return []
        seen.add(key)
        option: dict[str, Any] = {
            "key": key,
            "label": label,
            "display_order": item.get("display_order", index),
        }
        weight = item.get("weight")
        if isinstance(weight, (int, float)) and not isinstance(weight, bool):
            option["weight"] = float(weight)
        options.append(option)
    return options


def _normalize_dimension_config(
    value: Mapping[str, Any],
    *,
    allowed_dimension_keys: Iterable[str] = DIMENSION_KEYS,
) -> dict[str, Any]:
    unknown_fields = set(value) - {
        "enabled",
        "mode",
        "selected_keys",
        # category-pipeline-v1 originally exposed this name.  Keep accepting
        # and returning it while the frontend migrates to selected_keys.
        "enabled_keys",
    }
    if unknown_fields:
        raise ValueError(
            "维度配置包含未知字段：" + "、".join(sorted(unknown_fields))
        )

    raw_mode = value.get("mode")
    raw_enabled = value.get("enabled")
    if raw_enabled is not None and not isinstance(raw_enabled, bool):
        raise ValueError("维度 enabled 必须是布尔值")
    if raw_enabled is False and raw_mode in (None, "all", "none"):
        # category-pipeline-v1 clients historically only knew all/selected.
        # Treat their canonical disabled payload as the new explicit none mode.
        mode = "none"
    elif raw_mode is None:
        mode = "none" if raw_enabled is False else "all"
    elif isinstance(raw_mode, str) and raw_mode in DIMENSION_MODES:
        mode = raw_mode
    else:
        raise ValueError("维度模式必须是 all、selected 或 none")
    enabled = mode != "none" if raw_enabled is None else raw_enabled
    if enabled != (mode != "none"):
        raise ValueError("维度开关与模式不一致")

    selected_keys = value.get("selected_keys")
    legacy_keys = value.get("enabled_keys")
    if selected_keys is not None and legacy_keys is not None and selected_keys != legacy_keys:
        raise ValueError("维度 selected_keys 与 enabled_keys 不一致")
    keys = selected_keys if selected_keys is not None else legacy_keys
    if keys is None:
        keys = []
    if (
        not isinstance(keys, list)
        or len(keys) > 100
        or any(not isinstance(item, str) or not item for item in keys)
    ):
        raise ValueError("维度 selected_keys 必须是有效字符串列表")
    if len(keys) != len(set(keys)):
        raise ValueError("维度 selected_keys 不能重复")

    allowed = set(allowed_dimension_keys)
    unknown_dimension_keys = set(keys) - allowed
    if unknown_dimension_keys:
        raise ValueError(
            "包含未知维度指标："
            + "、".join(sorted(unknown_dimension_keys))
        )
    if mode == "selected" and not keys:
        raise ValueError("指定维度模式必须至少选择一个指标")
    if mode != "selected" and keys:
        raise ValueError(f"维度模式 {mode} 不能携带 selected_keys")

    canonical_keys = list(keys) if mode == "selected" else []
    return {
        "enabled": enabled,
        "mode": mode,
        "selected_keys": canonical_keys,
        "enabled_keys": canonical_keys,
    }


def dimension_selection_payload(
    pipeline: Mapping[str, Any],
    *,
    dimension_options: Iterable[Mapping[str, Any]] = DIMENSION_OPTIONS,
    schema_key: str | None = None,
    schema_version: str | None = None,
    schema_hash: str | None = None,
) -> dict[str, Any]:
    """Build the immutable per-job selection projection for one category."""

    options = [dict(item) for item in dimension_options]
    available_keys = [
        str(item["key"])
        for item in options
        if isinstance(item.get("key"), str) and item.get("key")
    ]
    dimensions = _normalize_dimension_config(
        pipeline.get("dimensions") or {},
        allowed_dimension_keys=available_keys,
    )
    mode = dimensions["mode"]
    effective_keys = (
        available_keys
        if mode == "all"
        else list(dimensions["selected_keys"])
        if mode == "selected"
        else []
    )
    return {
        "schema_version": "category-dimension-selection-v1",
        "enabled": dimensions["enabled"],
        "mode": mode,
        "selected_keys": list(dimensions["selected_keys"]),
        "effective_keys": effective_keys,
        "prompt_only": mode == "none",
        "source_schema": (
            {
                "schema_key": schema_key,
                "version": schema_version,
                "canonical_hash": schema_hash,
            }
            if schema_key is not None
            and schema_version is not None
            and schema_hash is not None
            else None
        ),
    }


def project_dimension_definition(
    definition: Mapping[str, Any],
    selection: Mapping[str, Any],
) -> dict[str, Any] | None:
    """Project an immutable published schema for one frozen job selection.

    The registry definition remains the source of truth and is never mutated.
    ``selected`` jobs receive a transient scoring projection with weights
    renormalized over the selected keys.  ``none`` deliberately returns no
    scoring contract because prompt-only runs must not enter dimension
    aggregation at all.
    """

    mode = selection.get("mode")
    effective_keys = selection.get("effective_keys")
    if mode not in DIMENSION_MODES:
        raise ValueError("冻结维度选择模式无效")
    if (
        not isinstance(effective_keys, list)
        or len(effective_keys) != len(set(effective_keys))
        or any(not isinstance(key, str) or not key for key in effective_keys)
    ):
        raise ValueError("冻结维度有效键无效")
    if mode == "none":
        if effective_keys:
            raise ValueError("关闭维度模式不能携带有效维度")
        return None

    projected = deepcopy(dict(definition))
    source_dimensions = projected.get("dimensions")
    output_contract = projected.get("output_contract")
    if not isinstance(source_dimensions, list) or not isinstance(output_contract, dict):
        raise ValueError("维度方案缺少维度或输出合同")
    by_key = {
        item.get("key"): item
        for item in source_dimensions
        if isinstance(item, dict) and isinstance(item.get("key"), str)
    }
    if set(effective_keys) - set(by_key):
        raise ValueError("冻结维度选择包含方案外指标")
    selected_dimensions = [deepcopy(by_key[key]) for key in effective_keys]
    if not selected_dimensions:
        raise ValueError("启用维度模式至少需要一个有效维度")

    scoring_dimensions = [
        item
        for item in selected_dimensions
        if item.get("aggregation_role") == "score"
    ]
    total_weight = sum(float(item.get("weight") or 0.0) for item in scoring_dimensions)
    if total_weight <= 0:
        raise ValueError("所选维度没有可用评分权重")
    for item in scoring_dimensions:
        item["weight"] = float(item["weight"]) / total_weight

    projected["dimensions"] = selected_dimensions
    output_contract["dimension_output_keys"] = list(effective_keys)
    projected["output_contract"] = output_contract
    core_keys = projected.get("core_dimension_keys")
    if isinstance(core_keys, list):
        projected["core_dimension_keys"] = [
            key for key in core_keys if key in effective_keys
        ]
    risk_review = projected.get("risk_review")
    if isinstance(risk_review, dict):
        risk_review["dimension_keys"] = list(effective_keys)

    # Eight-dimension collapse/evidence/L5 calibration cannot be applied to a
    # subset without changing its statistical meaning.  Keep deterministic
    # level thresholds and server caps, but fail closed on those full-set-only
    # heuristics by binding them to a non-matching profile marker.
    aggregation = projected.get("aggregation")
    if mode == "selected" and isinstance(aggregation, dict):
        collapse_rule = aggregation.get("collapse_rule")
        if isinstance(collapse_rule, dict):
            collapse_rule["same_grade_count_for_review"] = min(
                int(collapse_rule.get("same_grade_count_for_review") or 1),
                len(scoring_dimensions),
            )
            collapse_rule["applies_to_scoring_profile"] = (
                "__full_dimension_schema_only__"
            )
        high_evidence_rule = aggregation.get("high_evidence_rule")
        if isinstance(high_evidence_rule, dict):
            high_evidence_rule["dimensions_for_l3_cap"] = min(
                int(high_evidence_rule.get("dimensions_for_l3_cap") or 1),
                len(scoring_dimensions),
            )
        top_level_rule = aggregation.get("top_level_rule")
        if isinstance(top_level_rule, dict):
            top_level_rule["grade_five_minimum_count"] = min(
                int(top_level_rule.get("grade_five_minimum_count") or 1),
                len(scoring_dimensions),
            )
    projected["selection_projection"] = {
        "schema_version": "dimension-selection-projection-v1",
        "mode": mode,
        "effective_keys": list(effective_keys),
        "source_schema": deepcopy(selection.get("source_schema")),
        "weight_policy": (
            "source_weights"
            if mode == "all"
            else "renormalize_selected_to_one"
        ),
    }
    return projected


def dimension_selection_from_job_snapshot(
    snapshot: str | Mapping[str, Any] | None,
) -> dict[str, Any] | None:
    """Read a previously validated immutable job selection, if present."""

    if snapshot is None:
        return None
    if isinstance(snapshot, str):
        try:
            payload = json.loads(snapshot)
        except json.JSONDecodeError as exc:
            raise ValueError("任务冻结类目快照不是有效 JSON") from exc
    else:
        payload = dict(snapshot)
    if payload.get("schema_version") != "evaluation-category-profile-v2":
        return None
    selection = payload.get("dimension_selection")
    # v2 jobs accepted before category-dimension-selection-v1 are immutable
    # historical all-dimension runs.
    if selection is None:
        return None
    if not isinstance(selection, dict):
        raise ValueError("任务冻结类目快照维度选择损坏")
    if selection.get("schema_version") != "category-dimension-selection-v1":
        raise ValueError("任务冻结维度选择版本不受支持")
    mode = selection.get("mode")
    effective_keys = selection.get("effective_keys")
    if (
        mode not in DIMENSION_MODES
        or not isinstance(effective_keys, list)
        or len(effective_keys) != len(set(effective_keys))
        or any(not isinstance(key, str) or not key for key in effective_keys)
        or (mode == "none" and effective_keys)
        or (mode != "none" and not effective_keys)
    ):
        raise ValueError("任务冻结维度选择损坏")
    return deepcopy(selection)


def validate_pipeline_config(
    value: Mapping[str, Any],
    *,
    allowed_dimension_keys: Iterable[str] = DIMENSION_KEYS,
) -> dict[str, Any]:
    if not isinstance(value, Mapping) or value.get("schema_version") != "category-pipeline-v1":
        raise ValueError("类目流水线版本不受支持")
    input_kind = value.get("input_kind")
    if input_kind not in {"image", "pdf"}:
        raise ValueError("首期输入类型仅支持 image 或 pdf")
    processors = value.get("processors")
    if not isinstance(processors, list) or not processors:
        raise ValueError("类目至少需要一个处理模块")
    current_kind = input_kind
    normalized_processors: list[dict[str, Any]] = []
    seen: set[str] = set()
    for item in processors:
        if not isinstance(item, Mapping) or not isinstance(item.get("module"), str):
            raise ValueError("处理模块定义无效")
        module = str(item["module"])
        if module not in PROCESSOR_CATALOG:
            raise ValueError(f"未知处理模块：{module}")
        if module in seen:
            raise ValueError(f"处理模块不能重复：{module}")
        seen.add(module)
        enabled = item.get("enabled", True)
        if not isinstance(enabled, bool):
            raise ValueError(f"处理模块 {module}.enabled 必须是布尔值")
        config = _validate_config(module, item.get("config", {}))
        if enabled:
            accepted = PROCESSOR_CATALOG[module]["input_kinds"]
            if current_kind not in accepted:
                raise ValueError(f"处理模块 {module} 不能接收 {current_kind} 输入")
            current_kind = PROCESSOR_CATALOG[module]["output_kind"]
        normalized_processors.append({"module": module, "enabled": enabled, "config": config})
    active_modules = {item["module"] for item in normalized_processors if item["enabled"]}
    if input_kind == "image" and "image.prepare" not in active_modules:
        raise ValueError("图片流水线必须包含 image.prepare")
    if input_kind == "pdf":
        image_channel = {
            "document.page_contact_sheet",
            "document.page_batches",
        } & active_modules
        if "document.pdf_extract" not in active_modules or not image_channel:
            raise ValueError("PDF 流水线必须包含文本提取和一种页图输入通道")
    prompt_mode = value.get("prompt_mode", "single")
    if prompt_mode not in {"follow", "single", "ab"}:
        raise ValueError("提示词模式必须是 follow、single 或 ab")
    dimensions = value.get("dimensions", {})
    if not isinstance(dimensions, Mapping):
        raise ValueError("维度配置必须是对象")
    normalized_dimensions = _normalize_dimension_config(
        dimensions,
        allowed_dimension_keys=allowed_dimension_keys,
    )
    suffixes = value.get("allowed_suffixes", IMAGE_SUFFIXES if input_kind == "image" else PDF_SUFFIXES)
    if not isinstance(suffixes, list) or not suffixes or any(not isinstance(item, str) or not item.startswith(".") for item in suffixes):
        raise ValueError("允许后缀列表无效")
    allowed_suffixes = [item.lower() for item in dict.fromkeys(suffixes)]
    legal_suffixes = set(IMAGE_SUFFIXES if input_kind == "image" else PDF_SUFFIXES)
    if not set(allowed_suffixes).issubset(legal_suffixes):
        raise ValueError("文件后缀与输入类型不匹配")
    prompt_context = value.get("prompt_context", {})
    if not isinstance(prompt_context, Mapping) or not isinstance(prompt_context.get("instruction", ""), str):
        raise ValueError("提示词上下文配置无效")
    instruction = str(prompt_context.get("instruction", "")).strip()
    if len(instruction) > 2000:
        raise ValueError("类目附加指令不能超过 2000 字符")
    model_nodes = value.get("model_nodes", {})
    if not isinstance(model_nodes, Mapping) or any(not isinstance(v, bool) for v in model_nodes.values()):
        raise ValueError("模型节点开关无效")
    unknown_model_nodes = set(model_nodes) - set(MODEL_NODE_KEYS)
    if unknown_model_nodes:
        raise ValueError(f"包含未知模型节点：{', '.join(sorted(unknown_model_nodes))}")
    normalized_model_nodes = {
        key: bool(model_nodes.get(key, False)) for key in MODEL_NODE_KEYS
    }
    if not normalized_model_nodes["evaluation_main"]:
        raise ValueError("类目必须启用 evaluation_main 主评测节点")
    requires_summary = "document.multimodal_summary" in active_modules
    if requires_summary and not normalized_model_nodes["pdf_summary"]:
        raise ValueError("启用文档多模态总结时必须启用 pdf_summary 模型节点")
    return {
        "schema_version": "category-pipeline-v1",
        "input_kind": input_kind,
        "allowed_suffixes": allowed_suffixes,
        "processors": normalized_processors,
        "prompt_mode": prompt_mode,
        "prompt_context": {"instruction": instruction},
        "dimensions": normalized_dimensions,
        "model_nodes": normalized_model_nodes,
    }


def allowed_mimes_for_pipeline(pipeline: Mapping[str, Any]) -> tuple[str, ...]:
    return IMAGE_MIME_TYPES if pipeline.get("input_kind") == "image" else PDF_MIME_TYPES


def active_modules(pipeline: Mapping[str, Any]) -> set[str]:
    return {
        str(item["module"])
        for item in pipeline.get("processors", [])
        if isinstance(item, Mapping) and item.get("enabled", True)
    }


def processor_config(pipeline: Mapping[str, Any], module: str) -> dict[str, Any]:
    for item in pipeline.get("processors", []):
        if isinstance(item, Mapping) and item.get("module") == module and item.get("enabled", True):
            return dict(item.get("config") or {})
    return {}


def pipeline_catalog_payload() -> dict[str, Any]:
    return {
        "schema_version": "category-pipeline-catalog-v1",
        "input_kinds": [
            {"key": "image", "label": "图片 / 动图", "mime_types": list(IMAGE_MIME_TYPES), "suffixes": list(IMAGE_SUFFIXES)},
            {"key": "pdf", "label": "PDF 文档", "mime_types": list(PDF_MIME_TYPES), "suffixes": list(PDF_SUFFIXES)},
        ],
        "processors": [{"module": key, **deepcopy(value)} for key, value in PROCESSOR_CATALOG.items()],
        "dimension_options": deepcopy(list(DIMENSION_OPTIONS)),
        "dimension_modes": deepcopy(list(DIMENSION_MODE_CATALOG)),
        "dimension_config_contract": {
            "schema_version": "category-dimension-config-v1",
            "selected_key_field": "selected_keys",
            "legacy_selected_key_field": "enabled_keys",
            "published_schemas_immutable": True,
            "selection_is_frozen_per_job": True,
        },
        "model_nodes": [
            {"key": key, **deepcopy(value)}
            for key, value in MODEL_NODE_CATALOG.items()
        ],
        "prompt_modes": ["follow", "single", "ab"],
    }


def pipeline_json(pipeline: Mapping[str, Any]) -> str:
    return json.dumps(validate_pipeline_config(pipeline), ensure_ascii=False, sort_keys=True, separators=(",", ":"))

===== FILE backend/app/classification_level_map.py =====
"""分类定级表：规则模块类型 ``classification_level_map``。

运营文档（如《3D&SU模型分类定级规则》）给出「一级/二级/三级分类 → 分组类型 → 固定等级」表。
它是规则资产：随规则模块版本冻结、在规则模块页维护；收尾归并的规则层按提示词输出的
classification.doc_primary/doc_secondary/doc_tertiary 查表——命中固定等级的直接定级（不进美感打分），
没有固定等级的按分组落到赛道。提示词节点上的短路规则由本模块内容**生成**，只负责运行时跳过
后续模型调用，两层同源；提示词只负责输出分类字段，不承载规则。

内容合同（format_version = classification-level-map-v1）::

    {
      "format_version": "classification-level-map-v1",
      "enabled": true,
      "source": {"document_node": "..."},          # 可选，来源文档
      "field_paths": {"primary": "classification.doc_primary",
                      "secondary": "classification.doc_secondary",
                      "tertiary": "classification.doc_tertiary"},
      "rows": [{"primary": "交通工具", "secondary": "", "tertiary": "", "group": "功能性模型", "level": "L2"}],
      "group_track_map": {"空间/建筑类": "space_building", "软装配景": "soft_furnishing"}
    }
"""
from __future__ import annotations

from collections.abc import Mapping
from typing import Any

CLASSIFICATION_LEVEL_MAP_MODULE_TYPE = "classification_level_map"
CLASSIFICATION_LEVEL_MAP_FORMAT_VERSION = "classification-level-map-v1"
RULE_VERSION = "short-circuit-rule-v1"
_DEFAULT_FIELD_PATHS = {
    "primary": "classification.doc_primary",
    "secondary": "classification.doc_secondary",
    "tertiary": "classification.doc_tertiary",
}
_LEVELS = ("L1", "L2", "L3", "L4", "L5")


class ClassificationLevelMapError(ValueError):
    def __init__(self, code: str, message: str) -> None:
        super().__init__(message)
        self.code = code


def _text(value: Any) -> str:
    return value.strip() if isinstance(value, str) else ""


def validate_classification_level_map_content(content: dict[str, Any]) -> None:
    content.setdefault("format_version", CLASSIFICATION_LEVEL_MAP_FORMAT_VERSION)
    content.setdefault("enabled", True)
    content.setdefault("field_paths", dict(_DEFAULT_FIELD_PATHS))
    content.setdefault("rows", [])
    content.setdefault("group_track_map", {})
    if content["format_version"] != CLASSIFICATION_LEVEL_MAP_FORMAT_VERSION:
        raise ClassificationLevelMapError(
            "format_version_unsupported",
            f"分类定级表版本必须是 {CLASSIFICATION_LEVEL_MAP_FORMAT_VERSION}",
        )
    if not isinstance(content["enabled"], bool):
        raise ClassificationLevelMapError("enabled_invalid", "enabled 必须是布尔值")
    paths = content["field_paths"]
    if not isinstance(paths, Mapping) or any(
        not _text(paths.get(key)) for key in ("primary", "secondary", "tertiary")
    ):
        raise ClassificationLevelMapError("field_paths_invalid", "field_paths 必须含 primary/secondary/tertiary 三个路径")
    rows = content["rows"]
    if not isinstance(rows, list) or not rows:
        raise ClassificationLevelMapError("rows_invalid", "rows 必须是非空数组")
    seen: set[tuple[str, str, str]] = set()
    normalized_rows: list[dict[str, Any]] = []
    for index, row in enumerate(rows):
        if not isinstance(row, Mapping):
            raise ClassificationLevelMapError("row_invalid", f"rows[{index}] 必须是对象")
        primary, secondary, tertiary = (_text(row.get(k)) for k in ("primary", "secondary", "tertiary"))
        group, level = _text(row.get("group")), _text(row.get("level"))
        if not primary:
            raise ClassificationLevelMapError("row_primary_invalid", f"rows[{index}].primary 必须是非空字符串")
        if tertiary and not secondary:
            raise ClassificationLevelMapError("row_hierarchy_invalid", f"rows[{index}] 有三级分类却没有二级分类")
        if not group:
            raise ClassificationLevelMapError("row_group_invalid", f"rows[{index}].group 必须是非空字符串")
        if level and level not in _LEVELS:
            raise ClassificationLevelMapError("row_level_invalid", f"rows[{index}].level 只能是 L1-L5 或空")
        key = (primary, secondary, tertiary)
        if key in seen:
            raise ClassificationLevelMapError("row_duplicate", f"分类组合重复：{'/'.join(k for k in key if k)}")
        seen.add(key)
        normalized_rows.append({"primary": primary, "secondary": secondary, "tertiary": tertiary, "group": group, "level": level})
    content["rows"] = normalized_rows
    track_map = content["group_track_map"]
    if not isinstance(track_map, Mapping) or any(
        not isinstance(k, str) or not isinstance(v, str) for k, v in track_map.items()
    ):
        raise ClassificationLevelMapError("group_track_map_invalid", "group_track_map 必须是 分组→赛道标识 的字符串映射")
    content["group_track_map"] = {str(k): str(v) for k, v in track_map.items()}
    content["field_paths"] = {k: _text(paths.get(k)) for k in ("primary", "secondary", "tertiary")}


def _get_path(payload: Mapping[str, Any], path: str) -> str:
    current: Any = payload
    for part in path.split("."):
        if not isinstance(current, Mapping):
            return ""
        current = current.get(part)
    return _text(current)


def resolve_classification_level(
    content: Mapping[str, Any], precheck: Mapping[str, Any]
) -> dict[str, Any] | None:
    """按前检输出的三级分类查表。命中返回 {row, level, group, track_key, rule_key}，没命中返回 None。

    匹配语义：行的空二级/三级表示「无需拆分」，只要一级（及二级）相同即命中；
    有更具体（更多层级）的行时优先更具体的行。
    """
    if content.get("enabled", True) is not True:
        return None
    paths = content.get("field_paths") or _DEFAULT_FIELD_PATHS
    primary = _get_path(precheck, str(paths.get("primary", _DEFAULT_FIELD_PATHS["primary"])))
    secondary = _get_path(precheck, str(paths.get("secondary", _DEFAULT_FIELD_PATHS["secondary"])))
    tertiary = _get_path(precheck, str(paths.get("tertiary", _DEFAULT_FIELD_PATHS["tertiary"])))
    if not primary:
        return None
    best: dict[str, Any] | None = None
    best_depth = -1
    for row in content.get("rows", []) or []:
        if not isinstance(row, Mapping) or _text(row.get("primary")) != primary:
            continue
        row_secondary, row_tertiary = _text(row.get("secondary")), _text(row.get("tertiary"))
        if row_secondary and row_secondary != secondary:
            continue
        if row_tertiary and row_tertiary != tertiary:
            continue
        depth = int(bool(row_secondary)) + int(bool(row_tertiary))
        if depth > best_depth:
            best, best_depth = dict(row), depth
    if best is None:
        return None
    group = _text(best.get("group"))
    track_map = content.get("group_track_map") or {}
    return {
        "row": best,
        "level": _text(best.get("level")) or None,
        "group": group,
        "track_key": _text(track_map.get(group)) or None,
        "rule_key": classification_rule_key(best),
    }


def classification_rule_key(row: Mapping[str, Any]) -> str:
    parts = [_text(row.get(k)) for k in ("primary", "secondary", "tertiary")]
    return "clm:" + "/".join(part for part in parts if part)


def short_circuit_rules_from_classification_map(
    content: Mapping[str, Any],
    *,
    skip_roles: tuple[str, ...] = ("grading", "level_matching"),
    base_priority: int = 700,
) -> list[dict[str, Any]]:
    """由模块内容生成提示词节点的运行时短路规则（只为跳过后续模型调用，判定以本模块为准）。

    更具体的行优先级更高，避免「一级泛匹配」抢在「二级/三级精确行」之前命中。
    """
    if content.get("enabled", True) is not True:
        return []
    paths = content.get("field_paths") or _DEFAULT_FIELD_PATHS
    rules: list[dict[str, Any]] = []
    for row in content.get("rows", []) or []:
        level = _text(row.get("level")) if isinstance(row, Mapping) else ""
        if not level:
            continue
        conditions = [{"path": f"output.{paths['primary']}", "op": "eq", "value": _text(row.get("primary"))}]
        depth = 0
        for key in ("secondary", "tertiary"):
            value = _text(row.get(key))
            if value:
                conditions.append({"path": f"output.{paths[key]}", "op": "eq", "value": value})
                depth += 1
        rules.append(
            {
                "rule_key": classification_rule_key(row),
                "rule_version": RULE_VERSION,
                "priority": base_priority + depth * 10,
                "skip_roles": list(skip_roles),
                "target_level": level,
                "when": {"all": conditions} if len(conditions) > 1 else conditions[0],
                "derived_from": CLASSIFICATION_LEVEL_MAP_MODULE_TYPE,
            }
        )
    return rules

===== FILE backend/app/config.py =====
from __future__ import annotations

import os
import re
import sys
from dataclasses import dataclass
from functools import lru_cache
from pathlib import Path

from dotenv import load_dotenv


PROJECT_ROOT = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[2]
load_dotenv(PROJECT_ROOT / ".env")


def _default_data_dir(
    *,
    platform_name: str | None = None,
    home: Path | None = None,
) -> Path:
    effective_platform = platform_name or sys.platform
    if effective_platform == "darwin":
        return (home or Path.home()) / "Library" / "Application Support" / "3d66-label-system"
    local_app_data = os.getenv("LOCALAPPDATA")
    if local_app_data:
        return Path(local_app_data) / "3d66-label-system"
    return PROJECT_ROOT / "data"


def _production_feedback_token(data_dir: Path) -> str | None:
    environment_token = os.getenv("PRODUCTION_FEEDBACK_TOKEN", "").strip()
    if environment_token:
        return environment_token
    token_file = Path(
        os.getenv(
            "PRODUCTION_FEEDBACK_TOKEN_FILE",
            str(data_dir / "secrets" / "production-feedback.token"),
        )
    ).expanduser()
    if not token_file.is_file():
        return None
    token = token_file.read_text(encoding="utf-8").strip()
    return token or None


def _integration_token(
    data_dir: Path,
    *,
    environment_name: str,
    file_name: str,
) -> str | None:
    environment_token = os.getenv(environment_name, "").strip()
    if environment_token:
        return environment_token
    token_file = Path(
        os.getenv(
            f"{environment_name}_FILE",
            str(data_dir / "secrets" / file_name),
        )
    ).expanduser()
    if not token_file.is_file():
        return None
    token = token_file.read_text(encoding="utf-8").strip()
    return token or None


def _optional_path(environment_name: str) -> Path | None:
    """Read an optional mounted path without creating or probing it."""
    value = os.getenv(environment_name, "").strip()
    if not value:
        return None
    return Path(value).expanduser().resolve()


def _bounded_int(
    environment_name: str,
    *,
    default: int,
    minimum: int,
    maximum: int,
) -> int:
    raw_value = os.getenv(environment_name, str(default)).strip()
    try:
        value = int(raw_value)
    except ValueError as exc:
        raise ValueError(f"{environment_name} 必须是整数") from exc
    if not minimum <= value <= maximum:
        raise ValueError(
            f"{environment_name} 必须在 {minimum} 到 {maximum} 之间"
        )
    return value


def _optional_bounded_int(
    environment_name: str,
    *,
    maximum: int,
) -> int | None:
    raw_value = os.getenv(environment_name, "").strip()
    if not raw_value or raw_value == "0":
        return None
    return _bounded_int(
        environment_name,
        default=0,
        minimum=1,
        maximum=maximum,
    )


def _strict_optional_bounded_int(
    environment_name: str,
    *,
    maximum: int,
) -> int | None:
    raw_value = os.getenv(environment_name, "").strip()
    if not raw_value:
        return None
    return _bounded_int(
        environment_name,
        default=0,
        minimum=1,
        maximum=maximum,
    )


def _strict_bool(environment_name: str, *, default: bool) -> bool:
    raw_value = os.getenv(
        environment_name,
        "true" if default else "false",
    ).strip().lower()
    if raw_value == "true":
        return True
    if raw_value == "false":
        return False
    raise ValueError(f"{environment_name} 必须是 true 或 false")


def _database_url(data_dir: Path) -> str:
    environment_url = os.getenv("DATABASE_URL", "").strip()
    if environment_url:
        return environment_url
    secret_file = os.getenv("DATABASE_URL_FILE", "").strip()
    if secret_file:
        try:
            file_url = Path(secret_file).expanduser().read_text(
                encoding="utf-8"
            ).strip()
        except OSError as exc:
            raise ValueError("DATABASE_URL_FILE 无法读取") from exc
        if not file_url:
            raise ValueError("DATABASE_URL_FILE 不能为空")
        return file_url
    return f"sqlite:///{(data_dir / 'database' / 'app.db').as_posix()}"


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class Settings:
    project_root: Path
    data_dir: Path
    database_path: Path
    upload_dir: Path
    model_media_cache_root: Path
    nas_maps_root: Path | None
    nas_history_fallback_root: Path | None
    log_dir: Path
    prompt_source: Path
    frontend_dist: Path
    host: str
    port: int
    session_days: int
    production_feedback_token: str | None
    content_ingress_token: str | None
    connector_ingress_token: str | None
    label_consumer_token: str | None
    operations_metrics_token: str | None
    prompt_lab_token: str | None
    backup_status_file: Path
    restore_status_file: Path
    database_url: str
    sqlite_worker_limit: int
    worker_process_count: int | None
    worker_cluster_expected_count: int | None
    database_pool_size: int
    database_max_overflow: int
    deploy_environment: str
    session_cookie_secure: bool
    allow_insecure_session_cookie: bool
    session_cookie_name: str
    allow_default_admin_password: bool
    login_throttle_enabled: bool


@lru_cache(maxsize=1)
def get_settings() -> Settings:
    data_dir = Path(os.getenv("DATA_DIR", str(_default_data_dir()))).expanduser().resolve()
    deploy_environment = (
        os.getenv("LABEL_SYSTEM_DEPLOY_ENV", "local").strip().lower()
        or "local"
    )
    frontend_dist = Path(
        os.getenv("FRONTEND_DIST", str(PROJECT_ROOT / "frontend" / "dist"))
    ).expanduser().resolve()
    settings = Settings(
        project_root=PROJECT_ROOT,
        data_dir=data_dir,
        database_path=data_dir / "database" / "app.db",
        upload_dir=data_dir / "images",
        model_media_cache_root=Path(
            os.getenv(
                "MODEL_MEDIA_CACHE_ROOT",
                str(data_dir / "cache" / "model-media"),
            )
        ).expanduser().resolve(),
        nas_maps_root=_optional_path("NAS_MAPS_ROOT"),
        nas_history_fallback_root=_optional_path("NAS_HISTORY_FALLBACK_ROOT"),
        log_dir=data_dir / "logs",
        prompt_source=PROJECT_ROOT / "prompts" / "3d66-aesthetic-v2.1.md",
        frontend_dist=frontend_dist,
        host=os.getenv("APP_HOST", "0.0.0.0"),
        port=int(os.getenv("APP_PORT", "8080")),
        session_days=max(1, int(os.getenv("SESSION_DAYS", "7"))),
        production_feedback_token=_production_feedback_token(data_dir),
        content_ingress_token=_integration_token(
            data_dir,
            environment_name="CONTENT_INGRESS_TOKEN",
            file_name="content-ingress.token",
        ),
        connector_ingress_token=_integration_token(
            data_dir,
            environment_name="CONNECTOR_INGRESS_TOKEN",
            file_name="connector-ingress.token",
        ),
        label_consumer_token=_integration_token(
            data_dir,
            environment_name="LABEL_CONSUMER_TOKEN",
            file_name="label-consumer.token",
        ),
        operations_metrics_token=_integration_token(
            data_dir,
            environment_name="OPERATIONS_METRICS_TOKEN",
            file_name="operations-metrics.token",
        ),
        prompt_lab_token=_integration_token(
            data_dir,
            environment_name="PROMPT_LAB_TOKEN",
            file_name="prompt-lab.token",
        ),
        backup_status_file=(
            data_dir / "operations" / "last-successful-backup.json"
        ),
        restore_status_file=(
            data_dir / "operations" / "last-successful-restore.json"
        ),
        database_url=_database_url(data_dir),
        sqlite_worker_limit=max(1, int(os.getenv("SQLITE_WORKER_LIMIT", "2"))),
        worker_process_count=_optional_bounded_int(
            "WORKER_PROCESS_COUNT",
            maximum=32,
        ),
        worker_cluster_expected_count=_strict_optional_bounded_int(
            "WORKER_CLUSTER_EXPECTED_COUNT",
            maximum=10_000,
        ),
        database_pool_size=_bounded_int(
            "DATABASE_POOL_SIZE",
            default=2,
            minimum=1,
            maximum=20,
        ),
        database_max_overflow=_bounded_int(
            "DATABASE_MAX_OVERFLOW",
            default=0,
            minimum=0,
            maximum=20,
        ),
        deploy_environment=deploy_environment,
        # 同一主机上并行部署（正式 443/80 与测试 8082/8443）共用 Cookie 名会互相覆盖，
        # 且浏览器拒绝用 HTTP 响应改写已有的 Secure 同名 Cookie；测试服应配独立名称。
        session_cookie_name=(
            re.sub(r"[^A-Za-z0-9_-]", "", os.getenv("SESSION_COOKIE_NAME", "3d66_session").strip())
            or "3d66_session"
        ),
        allow_insecure_session_cookie=_strict_bool(
            "ALLOW_INSECURE_SESSION_COOKIE", default=False
        ),
        session_cookie_secure=_strict_bool(
            "SESSION_COOKIE_SECURE",
            default=False,
        ),
        allow_default_admin_password=_strict_bool(
            "ALLOW_DEFAULT_ADMIN_PASSWORD",
            default=deploy_environment != "production",
        ),
        # 2026-09-26 老大裁决：先去除登录限流。容器内只能看到 docker 网关地址，
        # 按来源限流等于全站共用一个桶；按账号限流又能被任何人锁住管理员。
        # 需要时设 LOGIN_THROTTLE_ENABLED=true 恢复。
        login_throttle_enabled=_strict_bool("LOGIN_THROTTLE_ENABLED", default=False),
    )
    settings.database_path.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    settings.upload_dir.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    settings.model_media_cache_root.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    settings.log_dir.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    settings.backup_status_file.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    settings.restore_status_file.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    return settings

===== FILE backend/app/connector_adapter_registry.py =====
"""Which connector adapters the platform can actually execute, and what each needs.

Registering a connector used to require someone who knew that
``schema_fingerprint`` is a 64-hex digest, which adapter key the delivery code
dispatches on, and where the digest comes from for that adapter - so every new
category went through an engineer. This registry is the missing half of a
visual configuration surface: the operator picks an adapter, and the platform
supplies the parts only it can know.

``executable`` is the honest bit. A contract can be registered with any adapter
key the regex allows, but only these keys are dispatched by
``connector_delivery.resolve_configured_downstream_adapter`` and the upstream
poll path. Anything else registers fine and then never delivers, which is
exactly the failure that is worth surfacing in the UI rather than at 3am.
"""
from __future__ import annotations

import hashlib
from dataclasses import dataclass


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class Adapter:
    key: str
    direction: str
    label: str
    summary: str
    #: 'constant' - fixed by the adapter and verified at delivery
    #: 'live_schema' - read from the real table behind the locator
    #: 'convention' - a fixed value the platform records but does not verify
    fingerprint_source: str
    locator_hint: str
    executable: bool
    constant_fingerprint: str | None = None
    #: Deployment-side environment the locator resolves through.
    environment_hint: str = ""


#: The factory list/confirm upstream has no live schema to read; the digest is a
#: registration convention, kept here so the two places that mint it agree.
FACTORY_LIST_CONFIRM_FINGERPRINT = hashlib.sha256(b'factory-list-confirm-material-v1').hexdigest()
#: Computed rather than imported: pulling it from factory_submit_delivery drags
#: the whole delivery chain into anything that just wants to list adapters. A
#: test asserts the two stay equal.
FACTORY_SUBMIT_FINGERPRINT = hashlib.sha256(b'factory-submit-result-v1').hexdigest()


ADAPTERS: tuple[Adapter, ...] = (
    Adapter(
        key='sql-envelope-v1',
        direction='downstream',
        label='写入数据库表',
        summary='把结果逐条写进一张投递信封表，按业务幂等键去重，投递后回读校验。',
        fingerprint_source='live_schema',
        locator_hint='logical.<名称>，部署侧配置 DSN 与物理表名',
        environment_hint='LABEL_SYSTEM_DATASOURCE_<定位符>[_TABLE|_SECRET_REFERENCE]',
        executable=True,
    ),
    Adapter(
        key='factory-submit-v1',
        direction='downstream',
        label='提交到工厂接口',
        summary='把打标结果 POST 到工厂 submit 接口，与 claim/confirmClaim 同一签名方案；服务端幂等。',
        fingerprint_source='constant',
        locator_hint='logical.<名称>，部署侧配置端点 URL，且必须是允许的工厂域名',
        environment_hint='LABEL_SYSTEM_ENDPOINT_<定位符>',
        executable=True,
        constant_fingerprint=FACTORY_SUBMIT_FINGERPRINT,
    ),
    Adapter(
        key='factory-test-list-confirm-v1',
        direction='upstream',
        label='工厂任务列表与认领',
        summary='从工厂接口列出待办任务并确认认领；claim 只读，confirmClaim 会转移归属。',
        fingerprint_source='convention',
        locator_hint='logical.<名称>，部署侧配置端点 URL',
        environment_hint='由进料配置指定上游域名',
        executable=True,
        constant_fingerprint=FACTORY_LIST_CONFIRM_FINGERPRINT,
    ),
    Adapter(
        key='manual-push-v1',
        direction='upstream',
        label='占位：外部推送',
        summary='占位合同，用于在真实上游接入前冻结工作流形状；平台不会真正读取任何数据。',
        fingerprint_source='convention',
        locator_hint='logical.placeholder.<名称>',
        executable=False,
    ),
    Adapter(
        key='local-sql-envelope-v1',
        direction='downstream',
        label='占位：本地模拟投递',
        summary='占位合同，写本地虚构表用于演练；不会写入公司业务库。',
        fingerprint_source='convention',
        locator_hint='logical.placeholder.<名称>',
        executable=False,
    ),
)

BY_KEY = {item.key: item for item in ADAPTERS}


def adapter(key: str) -> Adapter | None:
    return BY_KEY.get((key or '').strip())


def derive_fingerprint(key: str, logical_locator: str) -> str | None:
    """The digest the platform can supply itself, or None if it cannot.

    'live_schema' deliberately returns None: reading the real table needs the
    deployment-side DSN and table name, and a registration form is not the place
    to open that connection. The existing probe endpoint is.
    """
    item = adapter(key)
    if item is None:
        return None
    if item.fingerprint_source == 'constant':
        return item.constant_fingerprint
    if item.fingerprint_source == 'convention':
        return item.constant_fingerprint
    return None


def as_payload() -> list[dict[str, object]]:
    return [
        {
            'adapter_key': item.key,
            'direction': item.direction,
            'label': item.label,
            'summary': item.summary,
            'fingerprint_source': item.fingerprint_source,
            'fingerprint_supplied_by_platform': derive_fingerprint(item.key, '') is not None,
            'locator_hint': item.locator_hint,
            'environment_hint': item.environment_hint,
            'executable': item.executable,
        }
        for item in ADAPTERS
    ]

===== FILE backend/app/connector_contracts.py =====
from __future__ import annotations

import hashlib
import re
from collections.abc import Mapping, Sequence
from typing import Any

from sqlalchemy import func, select
from sqlalchemy.orm import Session

from .audit import canonical_json
from .models import ConnectorContractVersion


# Frozen workflows use dotted logical namespaces; each segment keeps the
# original identifier alphabet, with no empty segments or path separators.
_KEY = re.compile(r"^[a-z0-9][a-z0-9_-]*(?:\.[a-z0-9][a-z0-9_-]*)*$")
_CATEGORY_KEY = re.compile(r"^[a-z0-9][a-z0-9_-]{0,39}$")
_ADAPTER_KEY = re.compile(r"^[a-z0-9][a-z0-9_-]{0,79}$")
_LOGICAL_LOCATOR = re.compile(
    r"^logical\.[a-z0-9]+(?:[._-][a-z0-9]+)*$"
)
_SHA256 = re.compile(r"^[0-9a-f]{64}$")
_DIRECTIONS = {"upstream", "downstream"}
_STATUSES = {"draft", "active", "retired"}
_FORBIDDEN_KEYS = {
    "access_token",
    "api_key",
    "connection_string",
    "database_url",
    "dsn",
    "password",
    "physical_table",
    "query",
    "secret",
    "sql",
    "table",
    "token",
}
_FORBIDDEN_VALUE_PARTS = (
    "://",
    "password=",
    "token=",
    "secret=",
    "select ",
    "insert ",
    "update ",
    "delete ",
    "drop ",
    "alter ",
)


class ConnectorContractError(ValueError):
    def __init__(self, code: str, message: str) -> None:
        super().__init__(message)
        self.code = code


def _required_text(value: str, *, label: str) -> str:
    normalized = value.strip()
    if not normalized:
        raise ConnectorContractError(
            "connector_contract_invalid",
            f"{label}不能为空",
        )
    return normalized


def _safe_mapping(value: Mapping[str, Any], *, label: str) -> dict[str, Any]:
    if not isinstance(value, Mapping):
        raise ConnectorContractError(
            "connector_contract_invalid",
            f"{label}必须是对象",
        )

    def validate(item: Any, *, path: str) -> Any:
        if isinstance(item, Mapping):
            normalized: dict[str, Any] = {}
            for raw_key, raw_value in item.items():
                key = str(raw_key).strip()
                if not key:
                    raise ConnectorContractError(
                        "connector_contract_invalid",
                        f"{label}包含空字段名",
                    )
                if key.lower() in _FORBIDDEN_KEYS:
                    raise ConnectorContractError(
                        "connector_contract_sensitive_configuration",
                        f"{label}不得包含敏感配置：{path}{key}",
                    )
                normalized[key] = validate(
                    raw_value,
                    path=f"{path}{key}.",
                )
            return normalized
        if isinstance(item, Sequence) and not isinstance(
            item,
            (str, bytes, bytearray),
        ):
            return [
                validate(child, path=f"{path}{index}.")
                for index, child in enumerate(item)
            ]
        if isinstance(item, str):
            lowered = item.strip().lower()
            if any(part in lowered for part in _FORBIDDEN_VALUE_PARTS):
                raise ConnectorContractError(
                    "connector_contract_sensitive_configuration",
                    f"{label}不得包含敏感配置：{path.rstrip('.')}",
                )
            return item.strip()
        if item is None or isinstance(item, (bool, int, float)):
            return item
        raise ConnectorContractError(
            "connector_contract_invalid",
            f"{label}包含不支持的值：{path.rstrip('.')}",
        )

    return validate(value, path="")


def create_connector_contract_version(
    db: Session,
    *,
    contract_key: str,
    direction: str,
    category_key: str,
    logical_locator: str,
    adapter_key: str,
    schema_fingerprint: str,
    field_mapping: Mapping[str, Any],
    cursor_contract: Mapping[str, Any],
    idempotency_contract: Mapping[str, Any],
    batch_size: int,
    timeout_seconds: int,
    owner: str,
    status: str,
    created_by: str,
) -> ConnectorContractVersion:
    normalized_key = _required_text(contract_key, label="合同标识")
    normalized_direction = _required_text(direction, label="方向").lower()
    normalized_category = _required_text(category_key, label="类目标识")
    normalized_locator = _required_text(logical_locator, label="逻辑引用")
    normalized_adapter = _required_text(adapter_key, label="适配器标识")
    normalized_status = _required_text(status, label="状态").lower()
    normalized_fingerprint = schema_fingerprint.strip().lower()
    normalized_owner = _required_text(owner, label="负责人")
    normalized_creator = _required_text(created_by, label="创建人")

    if len(normalized_key) > 120 or not _KEY.fullmatch(normalized_key):
        raise ConnectorContractError(
            "connector_contract_invalid",
            "合同标识格式无效",
        )
    if normalized_direction not in _DIRECTIONS:
        raise ConnectorContractError(
            "connector_contract_invalid",
            "方向只能是上游或下游",
        )
    if not _CATEGORY_KEY.fullmatch(normalized_category):
        raise ConnectorContractError(
            "connector_contract_invalid",
            "类目标识格式无效",
        )
    if not _LOGICAL_LOCATOR.fullmatch(normalized_locator):
        raise ConnectorContractError(
            "connector_contract_physical_locator",
            "连接位置只能填写以 logical. 开头的逻辑引用",
        )
    if not _ADAPTER_KEY.fullmatch(normalized_adapter):
        raise ConnectorContractError(
            "connector_contract_invalid",
            "适配器标识格式无效",
        )
    if not _SHA256.fullmatch(normalized_fingerprint):
        raise ConnectorContractError(
            "connector_contract_invalid",
            "结构指纹必须是 SHA-256",
        )
    if normalized_status not in _STATUSES:
        raise ConnectorContractError(
            "connector_contract_invalid",
            "合同状态无效",
        )
    if isinstance(batch_size, bool) or not 1 <= batch_size <= 1000:
        raise ConnectorContractError(
            "connector_contract_invalid",
            "单批处理量必须在 1 到 1000 之间",
        )
    if isinstance(timeout_seconds, bool) or not 1 <= timeout_seconds <= 600:
        raise ConnectorContractError(
            "connector_contract_invalid",
            "超时时间必须在 1 到 600 秒之间",
        )

    normalized_mapping = _safe_mapping(field_mapping, label="字段映射")
    normalized_cursor = _safe_mapping(cursor_contract, label="游标规则")
    normalized_idempotency = _safe_mapping(
        idempotency_contract,
        label="去重规则",
    )
    definition = {
        "adapter_key": normalized_adapter,
        "batch_size": batch_size,
        "category_key": normalized_category,
        "contract_key": normalized_key,
        "cursor_contract": normalized_cursor,
        "direction": normalized_direction,
        "field_mapping": normalized_mapping,
        "idempotency_contract": normalized_idempotency,
        "logical_locator": normalized_locator,
        "owner": normalized_owner,
        "schema_fingerprint": normalized_fingerprint,
        "status": normalized_status,
        "timeout_seconds": timeout_seconds,
    }
    contract_hash = hashlib.sha256(
        canonical_json(definition).encode("utf-8")
    ).hexdigest()
    existing = db.scalar(
        select(ConnectorContractVersion).where(
            ConnectorContractVersion.contract_key == normalized_key,
            ConnectorContractVersion.contract_hash == contract_hash,
        )
    )
    if existing is not None:
        return existing

    latest_version = db.scalar(
        select(func.max(ConnectorContractVersion.version)).where(
            ConnectorContractVersion.contract_key == normalized_key
        )
    )
    contract = ConnectorContractVersion(
        contract_key=normalized_key,
        version=int(latest_version or 0) + 1,
        direction=normalized_direction,
        category_key=normalized_category,
        logical_locator=normalized_locator,
        adapter_key=normalized_adapter,
        schema_fingerprint=normalized_fingerprint,
        field_mapping_json=canonical_json(normalized_mapping),
        cursor_contract_json=canonical_json(normalized_cursor),
        idempotency_contract_json=canonical_json(normalized_idempotency),
        batch_size=batch_size,
        timeout_seconds=timeout_seconds,
        owner=normalized_owner,
        status=normalized_status,
        contract_hash=contract_hash,
        created_by=normalized_creator,
    )
    db.add(contract)
    db.flush()
    return contract

===== FILE backend/app/connector_delivery.py =====
from __future__ import annotations

import json
import os
import re
from collections.abc import Callable, Mapping, Sequence
from contextlib import AbstractContextManager
from dataclasses import dataclass
from datetime import datetime, timezone

from .downstream_publish_ledger import mark_downstream_publish_outcome
from .incremental_source_revision import source_revision_advances, source_revision_is_stale
from typing import Any, Protocol

from sqlalchemy import Connection, select, text
from sqlalchemy.orm import Session

from .audit import canonical_json
from .connector_governance import (
    connector_is_paused,
    paused_workflow_version_ids,
)
from .external_datasources import (
    ExternalDatasourceError,
    compute_live_schema_fingerprint,
    resolve_locator_secret_reference,
    resolve_locator_table,
    verify_least_privilege,
    writable_connection_factory,
)
from .models import (
    ConnectorContractVersion,
    RuntimeAuditEvent,
    RuntimeOutboxEvent,
)
from .runtime_outbox import (
    DeliveryReceipt,
    claim_outbox_event,
    eligible_outbox_events,
    complete_outbox_event,
    fail_outbox_event,

class ConnectorDeliveryError(RuntimeError):
    def __init__(self, code: str, message: str) -> None:
        super().__init__(message)
        self.code = code


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class DownstreamSafetyEvidence:
    least_privileged: bool
    schema_fingerprint: str
    verifier: str


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class DeliveryKey:
    business_idempotency_key: str
    mechanism_version_id: int
    payload_hash: str
    # ADR-0064：同一业务身份的第几版；review_state=reviewed 表示人工纠偏后重发
    revision: int = 1
    review_state: str = "auto"


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class DeliveryItem:
    event_id: int
    key: DeliveryKey
    payload: Mapping[str, Any]


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class DeliveryBatch:
    target_contract_key: str
    contract_version: int
    items: tuple[DeliveryItem, ...]


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class DeliveryWriteResult:
    key: DeliveryKey
    succeeded: bool
    external_record_key: str = ""
    error_code: str = ""
    retryable: bool = False
    # 上游自己的一句话说明（已清洗截断），只进审计详情：错误码要可枚举，这句不是。
    diagnostic: str = ""
    # 只读确认类重试要等下游落定，不能按秒级技术退避把次数用完；None 用投递默认退避。
    retry_after_seconds: float | None = None


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class DeliveryAttempt:
    results: tuple[DeliveryWriteResult, ...]


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class DeliveredRow:
    key: DeliveryKey
    external_record_key: str


def overwrite_verdict(
    stored: DeliveryKey, new: DeliveryKey, *, source_advanced: bool = False,
) -> str | None:
    """ADR-0064 覆盖护栏：返回拒绝码；None 表示允许原地覆盖。

    - 旧版本迟到（revision 更小）不能覆盖新版本；
    - 同一 revision 内容不同视为身份冲突（fail-closed）；
    - 自动结果只能在源内容已进入新版时覆盖旧版人工已审行。
    """

    if new.revision < stored.revision:
        return "downstream_stale_revision"
    if new.revision == stored.revision:
        return "downstream_identity_conflict"
    if (
        stored.review_state == "reviewed"
        and new.review_state != "reviewed"
        and not source_advanced
    ):
        return "downstream_review_guard"
    return None


OVERWRITE_REJECTION_CODES = frozenset(
    {"downstream_stale_revision", "downstream_review_guard"}
)


class DownstreamAdapter(Protocol):
    def verify_target(self) -> DownstreamSafetyEvidence: ...

    def deliver(self, batch: DeliveryBatch) -> DeliveryAttempt: ...

    def read_back(self, keys: Sequence[DeliveryKey]) -> tuple[DeliveredRow, ...]: ...


class FixtureDownstreamAdapter:
    def __init__(
        self,
        *,
        schema_fingerprint: str,
        least_privileged: bool = True,
        fail_once_keys: set[str] | None = None,
        corrupt_readback_keys: set[str] | None = None,
    ) -> None:
        self._evidence = DownstreamSafetyEvidence(
            least_privileged=least_privileged,
            schema_fingerprint=schema_fingerprint,
            verifier="fixture-explicit-least-privilege",
        )
        self._fail_once_keys = set(fail_once_keys or ())
        self._corrupt_readback_keys = set(corrupt_readback_keys or ())
        self._rows: dict[str, DeliveredRow] = {}
        self._payloads: dict[str, Mapping[str, Any]] = {}
        self.attempted_batches: list[tuple[str, ...]] = []

    @property
    def rows(self) -> tuple[DeliveredRow, ...]:
        return tuple(self._rows[key] for key in sorted(self._rows))

    def verify_target(self) -> DownstreamSafetyEvidence:
        return self._evidence

    def deliver(self, batch: DeliveryBatch) -> DeliveryAttempt:
        self.attempted_batches.append(
            tuple(item.key.business_idempotency_key for item in batch.items)
        )
        results: list[DeliveryWriteResult] = []
        for item in batch.items:
            business_key = item.key.business_idempotency_key
            if business_key in self._fail_once_keys:
                self._fail_once_keys.remove(business_key)
                results.append(
                    DeliveryWriteResult(
                        key=item.key,
                        succeeded=False,
                        error_code="downstream_transient_failure",
                        retryable=True,
                    )
                )
                continue
            existing = self._rows.get(business_key)
            if existing is not None and existing.key != item.key:
                previous_payload = self._payloads.get(business_key, {})
                verdict = (
                    "downstream_stale_revision"
                    if source_revision_is_stale(previous_payload, item.payload)
                    else overwrite_verdict(
                        existing.key, item.key,
                        source_advanced=source_revision_advances(previous_payload, item.payload),
                    )
                )
                if verdict is not None:
                    results.append(
                        DeliveryWriteResult(
                            key=item.key,
                            succeeded=False,
                            error_code=verdict,
                            retryable=False,
                        )
                    )
                    continue
            external_record_key = f"fixture:{business_key}"
            self._rows[business_key] = DeliveredRow(
                key=item.key,
                external_record_key=external_record_key,
            )
            self._payloads[business_key] = json.loads(canonical_json(dict(item.payload)))
            results.append(
                DeliveryWriteResult(
                    key=item.key,
                    succeeded=True,
                    external_record_key=external_record_key,
                )
            )
        return DeliveryAttempt(results=tuple(results))

    def read_back(self, keys: Sequence[DeliveryKey]) -> tuple[DeliveredRow, ...]:
        rows: list[DeliveredRow] = []
        for key in keys:
            row = self._rows.get(key.business_idempotency_key)
            if row is None:
                continue
            if key.business_idempotency_key in self._corrupt_readback_keys:
                row = DeliveredRow(
                    key=DeliveryKey(
                        business_idempotency_key=row.key.business_idempotency_key,
                        mechanism_version_id=row.key.mechanism_version_id,
                        payload_hash="0" * 64,
                    ),
                    external_record_key=row.external_record_key,
                )
            rows.append(row)
        return tuple(rows)


_IDENTIFIER = re.compile(r"^[A-Za-z_][A-Za-z0-9_]{0,62}$")


class SqlEnvelopeDownstreamAdapter:
    """Insert immutable delivery envelopes and reconcile them by business key."""

    def __init__(
        self,
        *,
        connection_factory: Callable[[], AbstractContextManager[Connection]],
        table_name: str,
        evidence: DownstreamSafetyEvidence,
    ) -> None:
        if not _IDENTIFIER.fullmatch(table_name):
            raise ConnectorDeliveryError(
                "downstream_target_invalid",
                "下游物理目标未通过标识符检查",
            )
        self._connection_factory = connection_factory
        self._table_name = table_name
        self._evidence = evidence

    def verify_target(self) -> DownstreamSafetyEvidence:
        return self._evidence

    @staticmethod
    def _stored_key(row: Mapping[str, Any]) -> DeliveryKey:
        return DeliveryKey(
            business_idempotency_key=str(row["business_idempotency_key"]),
            mechanism_version_id=int(row["mechanism_version_id"]),
            payload_hash=str(row["payload_hash"]),
            revision=int(row["revision"]),
            review_state=str(row["review_state"]),
        )

    def _select_one(
        self,
        connection: Connection,
        business_key: str,
    ) -> Mapping[str, Any] | None:
        quote = connection.dialect.identifier_preparer.quote
        return connection.execute(
            text(
                "SELECT business_idempotency_key, mechanism_version_id, "
                "payload_hash, payload_json, external_record_key, revision, review_state "
                f"FROM {quote(self._table_name)} "
                "WHERE business_idempotency_key = :business_key"
            ),
            {"business_key": business_key},
        ).mappings().one_or_none()

    def deliver(self, batch: DeliveryBatch) -> DeliveryAttempt:
        results: list[DeliveryWriteResult] = []
        with self._connection_factory() as connection:
            quote = connection.dialect.identifier_preparer.quote
            for item in batch.items:
                business_key = item.key.business_idempotency_key
                existing = self._select_one(connection, business_key)
                published_at = datetime.now(timezone.utc)
                if existing is not None:
                    stored_key = self._stored_key(existing)
                    if stored_key == item.key:
                        results.append(
                            DeliveryWriteResult(
                                key=item.key,
                                succeeded=True,
                                external_record_key=str(
                                    existing["external_record_key"]
                                ),
                            )
                        )
                        continue
                    try:
                        previous_payload = json.loads(existing["payload_json"])
                    except (TypeError, ValueError):
                        previous_payload = {}
                    if not isinstance(previous_payload, dict):
                        previous_payload = {}
                    verdict = (
                        "downstream_stale_revision"
                        if source_revision_is_stale(previous_payload, item.payload)
                        else overwrite_verdict(
                            stored_key, item.key,
                            source_advanced=source_revision_advances(previous_payload, item.payload),
                        )
                    )
                    if verdict is not None:
                        results.append(
                            DeliveryWriteResult(
                                key=item.key,
                                succeeded=False,
                                error_code=verdict,
                                retryable=False,
                            )
                        )
                        continue
                    # Compare the exact row used by the source/review precheck.
                    # A concurrent review must force readback reconciliation.
                    connection.execute(
                        text(
                            f"UPDATE {quote(self._table_name)} SET "
                            "mechanism_version_id = :mechanism_version_id, "
                            "payload_hash = :payload_hash, "
                            "payload_json = :payload_json, "
                            "revision = :revision, "
                            "review_state = :review_state, "
                            "published_at = :published_at "
                            "WHERE business_idempotency_key = :business_key "
                            "AND revision = :stored_revision "
                            "AND payload_hash = :stored_payload_hash "
                            "AND review_state = :stored_review_state "
                            "AND mechanism_version_id = :stored_mechanism_version_id"
                        ),
                        {
                            "business_key": business_key,
                            "mechanism_version_id": item.key.mechanism_version_id,
                            "payload_hash": item.key.payload_hash,
                            "payload_json": canonical_json(dict(item.payload)),
                            "revision": item.key.revision,
                            "review_state": item.key.review_state,
                            "published_at": published_at,
                            "stored_revision": stored_key.revision,
                            "stored_payload_hash": stored_key.payload_hash,
                            "stored_review_state": stored_key.review_state,
                            "stored_mechanism_version_id": stored_key.mechanism_version_id,
                        },
                    )
                else:
                    values = {
                        "business_key": business_key,
                        "mechanism_version_id": item.key.mechanism_version_id,
                        "payload_hash": item.key.payload_hash,
                        "payload_json": canonical_json(dict(item.payload)),
                        "external_record_key": business_key,
                        "revision": item.key.revision,
                        "review_state": item.key.review_state,
                        "published_at": published_at,
                    }
                    columns = (
                        "business_idempotency_key, mechanism_version_id, "
                        "payload_hash, payload_json, external_record_key, "
                        "revision, review_state, published_at"
                    )
                    parameters = (
                        ":business_key, :mechanism_version_id, :payload_hash, "
                        ":payload_json, :external_record_key, "
                        ":revision, :review_state, :published_at"
                    )
                    dialect = connection.dialect.name
                    if dialect in {"mysql", "mariadb"}:
                        statement = text(
                            f"INSERT IGNORE INTO {quote(self._table_name)} "
                            f"({columns}) VALUES ({parameters})"
                        )
                    elif dialect in {"sqlite", "postgresql"}:
                        statement = text(
                            f"INSERT INTO {quote(self._table_name)} ({columns}) "
                            f"VALUES ({parameters}) "
                            "ON CONFLICT(business_idempotency_key) DO NOTHING"
                        )
                    else:
                        raise ConnectorDeliveryError(
                            "downstream_adapter_unavailable",
                            "下游数据库类型尚无安全插入实现",
                        )
                    connection.execute(statement, values)
                stored = self._select_one(connection, business_key)
                if stored is None or self._stored_key(stored) != item.key:
                    results.append(
                        DeliveryWriteResult(
                            key=item.key,
                            succeeded=False,
                            error_code="downstream_identity_conflict",
                            retryable=False,
                        )
                    )
                    continue
                results.append(
                    DeliveryWriteResult(
                        key=item.key,
                        succeeded=True,
                        external_record_key=str(stored["external_record_key"]),
                    )
                )
        return DeliveryAttempt(results=tuple(results))

    def read_back(self, keys: Sequence[DeliveryKey]) -> tuple[DeliveredRow, ...]:
        rows: list[DeliveredRow] = []
        with self._connection_factory() as connection:
            for key in keys:
                stored = self._select_one(
                    connection,
                    key.business_idempotency_key,
                )
                if stored is None:
                    continue
                rows.append(
                    DeliveredRow(
                        key=self._stored_key(stored),
                        external_record_key=str(stored["external_record_key"]),
                    )
                )
        return tuple(rows)


def resolve_configured_downstream_adapter(
    target: ConnectorContractVersion,
) -> DownstreamAdapter:
    if target.direction == "downstream" and target.adapter_key == "inspiration-factory-submit-v1":
        from .inspiration_factory_delivery import resolve_inspiration_factory_adapter
        return resolve_inspiration_factory_adapter(target)
    if target.direction == "downstream" and target.adapter_key == "factory-submit-v1":
        # HTTPS submit to the factory; endpoint is deployment-side and allowlisted.
        from .factory_submit_delivery import resolve_factory_submit_adapter
        return resolve_factory_submit_adapter(target)
    if target.direction != "downstream" or target.adapter_key != "sql-envelope-v1":
        raise ConnectorDeliveryError(
            "downstream_adapter_unavailable",
            "该下游合同尚未配置可执行适配器",
        )
    try:
        secret_reference = resolve_locator_secret_reference(
            target.logical_locator
        )
        table_name = resolve_locator_table(target.logical_locator)
        least_privileged, verifier = verify_least_privilege(
            target.logical_locator,
            secret_reference,
            table_name=table_name,
        )
        deploy_env = (
            os.getenv("LABEL_SYSTEM_DEPLOY_ENV") or "production"
        ).strip().lower()
        if not least_privileged and deploy_env in {"local", "dev", "test", "ci"}:
            least_privileged = True
            verifier = f"{verifier}；仅非生产环境豁免"
        live_fingerprint = compute_live_schema_fingerprint(
            target.logical_locator,
            secret_reference,
            table_name=table_name,
        )
        connection_factory = writable_connection_factory(
            target.logical_locator,
            secret_reference,
        )
    except ExternalDatasourceError as exc:
        raise ConnectorDeliveryError(
            "downstream_adapter_unavailable",
            "下游连接尚未配置或无法通过安全检查",
        ) from exc
    return SqlEnvelopeDownstreamAdapter(
        connection_factory=connection_factory,
        table_name=table_name,
        evidence=DownstreamSafetyEvidence(
            least_privileged=least_privileged,
            schema_fingerprint=live_fingerprint,
            verifier=verifier,
        ),
    )


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class DeliveryBatchResult:
    target_contract_id: int
    claimed_event_ids: tuple[int, ...]
    delivered_event_ids: tuple[int, ...]
    retry_event_ids: tuple[int, ...]
    blocked_event_ids: tuple[int, ...]


def _validate_target(target: ConnectorContractVersion) -> None:
    if target.direction != "downstream":
        raise ConnectorDeliveryError(
            "downstream_contract_direction_invalid",
            "所选合同不是下游写入合同",
        )
    if target.status != "active":
        raise ConnectorDeliveryError(
            "downstream_contract_inactive",
            "下游写入合同未启用",
        )


def _key(event: RuntimeOutboxEvent) -> DeliveryKey:
    return DeliveryKey(
        business_idempotency_key=event.business_idempotency_key,
        mechanism_version_id=event.mechanism_version_id,
        payload_hash=event.payload_hash,
        revision=int(event.revision or 1),
        review_state=str(event.review_state or "auto"),
    )


def _audit_attempt(
    db: Session,
    event: RuntimeOutboxEvent,
    target: ConnectorContractVersion,
    *,
    worker_id: str,
    action: str,
    details: Mapping[str, Any],
) -> None:
    db.add(
        RuntimeAuditEvent(
            event_key=(
                f"outbox:{event.id}:attempt:{event.attempt_count}:{action}"
            ),
            entity_type="runtime_outbox_event",
            entity_key=str(event.id),
            action=action,
            actor=worker_id[:80],
            details_json=canonical_json(
                {
                    "target_contract_id": target.id,
                    "target_contract_key": target.contract_key,
                    "target_contract_version": target.version,
                    "business_idempotency_key": event.business_idempotency_key,
                    "mechanism_version_id": event.mechanism_version_id,
                    "payload_hash": event.payload_hash,
                    **dict(details),
                }
            ),
        )
    )


# 写入成功但读回尚未确认时，隔这么久再只读确认一次（10-01 事件 71215、78663：工厂落定滞后数秒）。
READBACK_CONFIRM_DELAY_SECONDS = 30.0


def _failure(
    *,
    error_code: str,
    retryable: bool,
    retry_after_seconds: float | None = None,
) -> RuntimeFailure:
    return RuntimeFailure(
        error_code=error_code,
        error_message="下游交付未通过逐条确认",
        classification="transient" if retryable else "contract",
        retryable=retryable,
        # None lets fail_outbox_event apply its backoff; 0 made a retryable
        # failure due again immediately, so a few seconds of downstream jitter
        # used up every attempt and dead-lettered the event.
        retry_after_seconds=retry_after_seconds,
    )


# A claimed batch holds its leases across adapter.deliver (up to batch_size
# requests, each allowed the full timeout) plus read_back. A lease of one
# request timeout expired mid-batch; completion then raised outbox_lease_stale
# and rolled back the whole transaction, re-submitting events the downstream
# had already accepted. Size the lease for the worst case, bounded so a crashed
# worker's claim still becomes recoverable within the hour.
DELIVERY_LEASE_MARGIN_SECONDS = 60
MAX_DELIVERY_LEASE_SECONDS = 3600


def _delivery_lease_seconds(target: ConnectorContractVersion) -> int:
    worst = target.timeout_seconds * (target.batch_size + 1) + DELIVERY_LEASE_MARGIN_SECONDS
    return max(target.timeout_seconds, min(worst, MAX_DELIVERY_LEASE_SECONDS))


def deliver_outbox_batch(
    db: Session,
    target: ConnectorContractVersion,
    *,
    worker_id: str,
    adapter: DownstreamAdapter,
    now: datetime | None = None,
) -> DeliveryBatchResult:
    _validate_target(target)
    if not worker_id.strip():
        raise ValueError("worker_id 不能为空")
    if connector_is_paused(
        db,
        category_key=target.category_key,
        mechanism_key="",
        contract_key=target.contract_key,
    ):
        raise ConnectorDeliveryError(
            "downstream_connector_paused",
            "该下游接口已暂停交付",
        )
    from .incremental_routing import fence_outbox_payload
    from .workflow_runtime import WorkflowRuntimeError
    excluded_versions = set(paused_workflow_version_ids(db,
        category_key=target.category_key, contract_key=target.contract_key))
    blocked_ids: list[int] = []

    def quarantine(event, error):
        fail_outbox_event(db, event.id, event.lease_token,
            _failure(error_code=error.code, retryable=False), now=now)
        mark_downstream_publish_outcome(db, outbox_event_id=event.id,
            delivery_status="rejected", rejection_code=error.code, now=now)
        _audit_attempt(db, event, target, worker_id=worker_id,
            action="delivery_fenced", details={"error_code": error.code,
                "resulting_status": "dead_lettered", "formal_write_performed": False})
        blocked_ids.append(event.id)
        db.flush()

    candidates = eligible_outbox_events(db, now=now, limit=target.batch_size,
        target_contract_key=target.contract_key, target_contract_version_id=target.id,
        excluded_mechanism_version_ids=excluded_versions)
    safe_ids: set[int] = set()
    for candidate in candidates:
        try:
            fence_outbox_payload(db, load_snapshot_blob(candidate.payload_blob))
        except WorkflowRuntimeError as exc:
            event = claim_outbox_event(db, worker_id, now=now,
                lease_seconds=_delivery_lease_seconds(target), only_event_ids={candidate.id},
                excluded_mechanism_version_ids=excluded_versions)
            if event is not None:
                quarantine(event, exc)
        else:
            safe_ids.add(candidate.id)
    if not safe_ids:
        return DeliveryBatchResult(target.id, (), (), (), tuple(blocked_ids))
    try:
        evidence = adapter.verify_target()
        if not evidence.least_privileged:
            raise ConnectorDeliveryError(
                "downstream_permission_overbroad",
                "下游账号权限超出合同范围",
            )
        if evidence.schema_fingerprint != target.schema_fingerprint:
            raise ConnectorDeliveryError(
                "downstream_schema_drift",
                "下游结构与冻结合同不一致",
            )
    except ConnectorDeliveryError as exc:
        if not blocked_ids:
            raise
        # Preserve the quarantine in the caller's commit. Healthy rows remain
        # unclaimed; the next tick reports the target error independently.
        for candidate in candidates:
            if candidate.id in blocked_ids:
                _audit_attempt(db, candidate, target, worker_id=worker_id,
                    action="delivery_target_blocked", details={"error_code": exc.code})
        db.flush()
        return DeliveryBatchResult(target.id, (), (), (), tuple(blocked_ids))

    excluded_versions = set(
        paused_workflow_version_ids(
            db,
            category_key=target.category_key,
            contract_key=target.contract_key,
        )
    )
    claimed: list[RuntimeOutboxEvent] = []
    for _ in range(target.batch_size):
        event = claim_outbox_event(
            db,
            worker_id,
            now=now,
            lease_seconds=_delivery_lease_seconds(target),
            target_contract_key=target.contract_key,
            target_contract_version_id=target.id,
            excluded_mechanism_version_ids=excluded_versions,
            only_event_ids=safe_ids,
        )
        if event is None:
            break
        mechanism_key = f"workflow-version:{event.mechanism_version_id}"
        if connector_is_paused(
            db,
            category_key=target.category_key,
            mechanism_key=mechanism_key,
            contract_key=target.contract_key,
        ):
            lease_token = event.lease_token
            if lease_token is None:
                raise ConnectorDeliveryError(
                    "downstream_lease_lost",
                    "下游交付租约丢失",
                )
            release_outbox_claim(db, event.id, lease_token, now=now)
            excluded_versions.add(event.mechanism_version_id)
            continue
        claimed.append(event)
    if not claimed:
        return DeliveryBatchResult(target.id, (), (), (), tuple(blocked_ids))

    deliverable: list[RuntimeOutboxEvent] = []
    for event in claimed:
        if connector_is_paused(
            db,
            category_key=target.category_key,
            mechanism_key=f"workflow-version:{event.mechanism_version_id}",
            contract_key=target.contract_key,
        ):
            lease_token = event.lease_token
            if lease_token is None:
                raise ConnectorDeliveryError(
                    "downstream_lease_lost",
                    "下游交付租约丢失",
                )
            release_outbox_claim(db, event.id, lease_token, now=now)
            continue
