    if int(getattr(evaluation, "review_revision", 0) or 0) != review_revision:
        raise CorrectionViewError(
            409,
            "CORRECTION_REVIEW_STALE",
            "审核修订号已变化，请刷新后重试",
        )

    paid_rerun_required = False
    production_run_id = getattr(evaluation, "production_run_id", None)
    if isinstance(production_run_id, int):
        from .workflow_correction import correction_paid_prompt_descendants

        production_run = db.get(ProductionRun, production_run_id)
        if (
            production_run is not None
            and production_run.model_execution_mode == "real"
        ):
            for contract_node, _request_node in prepared:
                metadata = contract_node.get("metadata")
                node_instance_id = (
                    str(metadata.get("node_instance_id") or "")
                    if isinstance(metadata, Mapping)
                    else ""
                )
                if node_instance_id and correction_paid_prompt_descendants(
                    production_run, node_instance_id
                ):
                    paid_rerun_required = True
                    break
    if paid_rerun_required and not real_execution_confirmed:
        raise CorrectionViewError(
            409,
            "CORRECTION_PAID_RERUN_CONFIRMATION_REQUIRED",
            "本次修改会改变下游提示词输入；请明确确认真实模型费用后再提交",
        )
    if paid_rerun_required and len(prepared) != 1:
        raise CorrectionViewError(
            422,
            "CORRECTION_PAID_RERUN_SINGLE_NODE_REQUIRED",
            "涉及真实模型重跑时请一次只提交一个节点，避免重复计费和中间状态歧义",
        )

    savepoint = None if paid_rerun_required else db.begin_nested()
    try:
        for contract_node, request_node in prepared:
            node_key = str(contract_node["node_key"])
            current_roots = _current_roots(item)
            authoritative_values = _values_for_contract(
                current_roots, {"nodes": [contract_node]}
            )
            prior_human = _human_values(
                item,
                runtime_contract,
                frozen_contract_hash=frozen_hash,
            ).get(node_key)
            current_value = (
                deepcopy(prior_human.get("human_value"))
                if isinstance(prior_human, Mapping)
                and "human_value" in prior_human
                else authoritative_values.get(node_key)
            )
            original_evidence = deepcopy(request_node.get("evidence", []))
            correction_key = "contract:" + hashlib.sha256(
                f"{idempotency_key}:{node_key}".encode("utf-8")
            ).hexdigest()[:48]
            metadata = contract_node.get("metadata")
            workflow_node_key = (
                str(metadata.get("workflow_node_key") or "")
                if isinstance(metadata, Mapping)
                else ""
            )
            node_instance_id = (
                str(metadata.get("node_instance_id") or workflow_node_key)
                if isinstance(metadata, Mapping)
                else workflow_node_key
            )
            if workflow_node_key:
                production_run_id = getattr(
                    evaluation, "production_run_id", None
                )
                if isinstance(production_run_id, int):
                    from .workflow_correction import (
                        WorkflowCorrectionError,
                        append_node_correction_and_replay,
                    )

                    field_path = str(
                        metadata.get("output_field_key")
                        or metadata.get("module_field_key")
                        or "output"
                    )
                    try:
                        append_node_correction_and_replay(
                            db,
                            run_id=production_run_id,
                            node_instance_id=node_instance_id,
                            field_path=field_path,
                            value=deepcopy(request_node.get("human_value")),
                            reason=str(
                                request_node.get("reason") or ""
                            ).strip(),
                            actor=actor,
                            correction_key=correction_key,
                            evidence=_normalized_evidence(
                                node_key, original_evidence
                            ),
                            event_metadata={
                                "node_path": str(
                                    contract_node.get("path")
                                    or contract_node["node_key"]
                                ),
                                "run_id": getattr(run, "id", None),
                            },
                            foreach_item_key=(
                                str(metadata.get("foreach_item_key"))
                                if isinstance(
                                    metadata.get("foreach_item_key"), str
                                )
                                else None
                            ),
                            foreach_item_field_path=(
                                str(
                                    metadata.get("foreach_item_field_path")
                                )
                                if isinstance(
                                    metadata.get("foreach_item_field_path"),
                                    str,
                                )
                                else None
                            ),
                            real_execution_confirmed=(
                                real_execution_confirmed
                            ),
                        )
                    except WorkflowCorrectionError as exc:
                        raise CorrectionViewError(
                            exc.status_code,
                            exc.code,
                            str(exc),
                            fields=[node_key],
                        ) from exc
                else:
                    history = _json_list(evaluation.correction_history_json)
                    prior = _human_values(
                        item,
                        runtime_contract,
                        frozen_contract_hash=frozen_hash,
                    ).get(node_key)
                    old_value = (
                        deepcopy(prior.get("human_value"))
                        if isinstance(prior, Mapping)
                        and "human_value" in prior
                        else current_value
                    )
                    history.append(
                        {
                            "correction_key": correction_key,
                            "node_type": "workflow_node",
                            "node_path": str(
                                contract_node.get("path")
                                or contract_node["node_key"]
                            ),
                            "old_value": old_value,
                            "new_value": deepcopy(
                                request_node.get("human_value")
                            ),
                            "evidence": _normalized_evidence(
                                node_key, original_evidence
                            ),
                            "reason": str(
                                request_node.get("reason") or ""
                            ).strip(),
                            "reason_codes": [],
                            "corrector": actor,
                            "corrected_at": platform_isoformat(datetime.now(timezone.utc)),
                            "downstream_recomputed": False,
                            "workflow_node_key": workflow_node_key,
                            "node_instance_id": node_instance_id,
                            "workflow_kind": metadata.get("workflow_kind"),
                            "run_id": getattr(run, "id", None),
                        }
                    )
                    evaluation.correction_history_json = json.dumps(
                        history, ensure_ascii=False
                    )
                    db.flush()
            else:
                apply_node_correction(
                    db,
                    result=evaluation,
                    payload=CorrectNodeRequest(
                        correction_key=correction_key,
                        node_type=_node_runtime_type(contract_node),
                        node_path=str(
                            contract_node.get("path") or contract_node["node_key"]
                        ),
                        old_value=current_value,
                        new_value=deepcopy(request_node.get("human_value")),
                        evidence=_normalized_evidence(node_key, original_evidence),
                        reason=str(request_node.get("reason") or "").strip(),
                    ),
                    corrector=actor,
                )
            history = _json_list(evaluation.correction_history_json)
            event = next(
                (
                    entry
                    for entry in reversed(history)
                    if isinstance(entry, dict)
                    and entry.get("correction_key") == correction_key
                ),
                None,
            )
            if event is None:
                raise CorrectionViewError(
                    409,
                    "CORRECTION_HISTORY_APPEND_FAILED",
                    f"节点 {node_key} 未形成可审计的纠偏历史",
                    fields=[node_key],
                )
            event.update(
                {
                    "schema_version": "contract-node-correction-v1",
                    "node_key": node_key,
                    "old_value": deepcopy(current_value),
                    "contract_hash": contract_hash,
                    "idempotency_key": idempotency_key,
                    "submission_hash": submission_hash,
                    "submitted_review_revision": review_revision,
                    "resulting_review_revision": review_revision + 1,
                    "contract_evidence": original_evidence,
                    **(
                        {"workflow_node_key": workflow_node_key}
                        if workflow_node_key
                        else {}
                    ),
                    **(
                        {
                            "node_instance_id": node_instance_id,
                            "workflow_kind": metadata.get("workflow_kind"),
                            "module_type_key": metadata.get(
                                "module_type_key"
                            ),
                            "module_field_key": metadata.get(
                                "module_field_key"
                            ),
                            "output_field_key": metadata.get(
                                "output_field_key"
                            ),
                            "foreach_item_key": metadata.get(
                                "foreach_item_key"
                            ),
                            "foreach_item_field_path": metadata.get(
                                "foreach_item_field_path"
                            ),
                            "field_identity": deepcopy(
                                metadata.get("field_identity")
                            ),
                            "run_id": getattr(run, "id", None),
                            "workflow_definition_id": (
                                contract.get("workflow", {}).get(
                                    "workflow_definition_id"
                                )
                                if isinstance(
                                    contract.get("workflow"), Mapping
                                )
                                else None
                            ),
                            "workflow_version_id": (
                                contract.get("workflow", {}).get(
                                    "workflow_version_id"
                                )
                                if isinstance(
                                    contract.get("workflow"), Mapping
                                )
                                else None
                            ),
                            "workflow_canonical_hash": (
                                contract.get("workflow", {}).get(
                                    "canonical_hash"
                                )
                                if isinstance(
                                    contract.get("workflow"), Mapping
                                )
                                else None
                            ),
                            "frozen_asset_identity": {
                                key: deepcopy(metadata[key])
                                for key in (
                                    "component_version",
                                    "prompt_version",
                                    "model_version",
                                    "rule_version",
                                    "selected_parameters",
                                )
                                if key in metadata
                            },
                        }
                        if workflow_node_key
                        and isinstance(metadata, Mapping)
                        else {}
                    ),
                }
            )
            evaluation.correction_history_json = json.dumps(
                history, ensure_ascii=False
            )
            if (
                not workflow_node_key
                and _node_runtime_type(contract_node) == "final_level"
                and getattr(run, "baseline_set_id", None) is not None
            ):
                category_key = (
                    getattr(run, "category_key", None)
                    or
                    getattr(getattr(evaluation, "job", None), "category_key", None)
                    or getattr(getattr(evaluation, "asset", None), "category_key", None)
                )
                if isinstance(category_key, str) and category_key:
                    persist_human_level_truth(
                        db,
                        category_key=category_key,
                        asset_id=int(evaluation.asset_id),
                        source_result_id=int(evaluation.id),
                        level=str(request_node.get("human_value")),
                        actor=actor,
                        reason=str(request_node.get("reason") or "人工纠偏更新最终等级"),
                    )
        evaluation.review_revision = review_revision + 1
        db.flush()
        if savepoint is not None:
            savepoint.commit()
    except Exception:
        if savepoint is not None:
            savepoint.rollback()
        else:
            db.rollback()
        raise

    return build_correction_view(db, run=run, item=item)

===== FILE backend/app/cover_attempts.py =====
"""Durable generation attempts, independent from HTTP request lifetimes.

Workers must claim an attempt before invoking ``advance``. The submission intent
is committed before any gateway POST: recovery always queries that same ID.
This module does not activate workflows or send results to business downstreams.
"""
from __future__ import annotations

from datetime import datetime, timezone, timedelta
import hashlib
import json
from pathlib import Path
import uuid

from sqlalchemy import Boolean, Integer, String, Text, UniqueConstraint, select, update
from sqlalchemy.orm import Mapped, Session, mapped_column

from .database import Base
from .cover_gateway import CoverGateway, CoverGatewayError, CoverParameters, _image_info, store_content


class CoverGenerationAttempt(Base):
    __tablename__ = "cover_generation_attempts"
    __table_args__ = (UniqueConstraint("sample_key", "sequence_no", name="uq_cover_sample_sequence"),)
    id: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(64), primary_key=True)
    sample_key: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(160), index=True)
    sequence_no: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(Integer)
    state: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(32), index=True)
    input_json: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(Text)
    uploaded_image: Mapped[str | None] = mapped_column(String(80), nullable=True)
    prompt_id: Mapped[str | None] = mapped_column(String(100), nullable=True)
    result_json: Mapped[str | None] = mapped_column(Text, nullable=True)
    error_code: Mapped[str | None] = mapped_column(String(80), nullable=True)
    retry_count: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(Integer, default=0, server_default="0")
    retry_after: Mapped[str | None] = mapped_column(String(40), nullable=True)
    gateway_unsettled: Mapped[bool] = mapped_column(Boolean, default=False, server_default="0")
    resolution_json: Mapped[str | None] = mapped_column(Text, nullable=True)
    created_at: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(40))
    updated_at: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(40))


def _now():
    return datetime.now(timezone.utc).isoformat()


def create_attempt(db: Session, *, sample_key: str, sequence_no: int, image: bytes,
                   prompt: str, parameters: CoverParameters, input_directory: Path) -> CoverGenerationAttempt:
    """Freeze source bytes before queueing. Caller commits its enclosing transaction."""
    if not sample_key or len(sample_key) > 160 or type(sequence_no) is not int or sequence_no < 1:
        raise CoverGatewayError("invalid_sample_identity")
    parameters.validate()
    if not isinstance(prompt, str) or not prompt.strip() or len(prompt) > 8000:
        raise CoverGatewayError("invalid_prompt")
    _image_info(image)
    digest = hashlib.sha256(image).hexdigest()
    source = input_directory / digest
    store_content(image, source)
    snapshot = {"image_sha256": digest, "prompt": prompt, "parameters": vars(parameters),
                "provider": "cover-image-lan-v1", "mode": "image_edit"}
    now = _now()
    attempt = CoverGenerationAttempt(id="cover-" + uuid.uuid4().hex, sample_key=sample_key,
        sequence_no=sequence_no, state="prepared", input_json=json.dumps(snapshot, ensure_ascii=False),
        created_at=now, updated_at=now)
    db.add(attempt)
    db.flush()
    return attempt


def _transition(db: Session, attempt_id: str, source: str, target: str, **values) -> bool:
    changed = db.execute(update(CoverGenerationAttempt).where(
        CoverGenerationAttempt.id == attempt_id, CoverGenerationAttempt.state == source
    ).values(state=target, updated_at=_now(), **values)).rowcount
    db.commit()
    return changed == 1


def _network_error(db: Session, row: CoverGenerationAttempt, source: str,
                   retry_target: str, exc: CoverGatewayError, *, rejected_submission=False) -> str:
    retries = row.retry_count + 1
    target = retry_target if exc.retryable and retries <= 3 else "needs_review"
    retry_after = (datetime.now(timezone.utc) + timedelta(seconds=5 * 2 ** min(retries - 1, 3))).isoformat()
    values = {"retry_count": retries, "retry_after": retry_after, "error_code": exc.code}
    # A rejected queue submission has no unresolved generation job.
    if rejected_submission:
        values["gateway_unsettled"] = False
    _transition(db, row.id, source, target, **values)
    return target


def request_recheck(db: Session, attempt_id: str) -> None:
    """Queue a GET of the original ID. This never grants permission for a POST."""
    row = db.scalar(select(CoverGenerationAttempt).where(CoverGenerationAttempt.id == attempt_id)
                    .with_for_update().execution_options(populate_existing=True))
    if row is None or row.state != "needs_review" or not row.uploaded_image:
        raise CoverGatewayError("attempt_not_recheckable")
    if not row.gateway_unsettled and not (row.error_code or "").startswith(("download_", "stored_image_")):
        raise CoverGatewayError("attempt_not_recheckable")
    row.state = "submitting" if row.gateway_unsettled else "download_pending"
    row.retry_count = 0
    row.retry_after = None
    row.updated_at = _now()
    db.flush()


def can_retry(row: CoverGenerationAttempt) -> bool:
    return row.state == "needs_review" and not row.gateway_unsettled and (
        row.error_code in {"queue_full", "gateway_unreachable"} or
        (row.error_code or "").startswith("gateway_http_5"))


def request_retry(db: Session, attempt_id: str) -> None:
    row = db.scalar(select(CoverGenerationAttempt).where(CoverGenerationAttempt.id == attempt_id)
                    .with_for_update().execution_options(populate_existing=True))
    if row is None or not can_retry(row):
        raise CoverGatewayError("original_attempt_not_reconciled")
    row.state = "ready" if row.uploaded_image else "prepared"
    row.retry_count = 0
    row.retry_after = None
    row.updated_at = _now()
    db.flush()


def can_regenerate(row: CoverGenerationAttempt) -> bool:
    return not row.gateway_unsettled and (row.state in {"succeeded", "failed"} or (
        row.state == "needs_review" and row.error_code not in {
            "submission_unknown", "history_unavailable", "job_not_found", "gateway_invalid_job", "gateway_invalid_response"}))


def resolve_unknown(db: Session, attempt_id: str, *, actor: str, evidence: str, expected_updated_at: str) -> None:
    """Record an operator's investigation, never infer absence from a timeout."""
    row = db.scalar(select(CoverGenerationAttempt).where(CoverGenerationAttempt.id == attempt_id)
                    .with_for_update().execution_options(populate_existing=True))
    if row is None or row.state != "needs_review" or not row.gateway_unsettled:
        raise CoverGatewayError("attempt_not_unresolved")
    if row.updated_at != expected_updated_at:
        raise CoverGatewayError("attempt_changed")
    if not evidence.strip() or len(evidence) > 2000:
        raise CoverGatewayError("investigation_evidence_required")
    row.resolution_json = json.dumps({"actor":actor,"evidence":evidence.strip(),"at":_now(),
        "statement":"operator_confirmed_original_job_not_running", "previous_error":row.error_code,
        "prompt_id":row.prompt_id}, ensure_ascii=False)
    row.state = "failed"; row.gateway_unsettled = False
    row.error_code = "operator_confirmed_job_closed"; row.updated_at = _now()
    from .audit import append_audit_event
    append_audit_event(db, category="cover_generation", action="resolve_unknown", subject_type="attempt",
        subject_id=row.id, actor=actor, payload=json.loads(row.resolution_json))
    db.flush()


def _download(db: Session, row: CoverGenerationAttempt, gateway: CoverGateway, directory: Path) -> str:
    if not _transition(db, row.id, "download_pending", "downloading", gateway_unsettled=False):
        return "contended"
    try:
        stored = gateway.download(row.id, directory=directory)
    except (CoverGatewayError, OSError) as exc:
        if not isinstance(exc, CoverGatewayError):
            exc = CoverGatewayError("download_storage_unavailable", retryable=True)
        return _network_error(db, row, "downloading", "download_pending", exc)
    result = {"sha256":stored.sha256,"bytes":stored.size_bytes,"width":stored.width,"height":stored.height}
    _transition(db, row.id, "downloading", "succeeded", result_json=json.dumps(result),
                error_code=None,retry_count=0,retry_after=None,gateway_unsettled=False)
    return "succeeded"


def advance(db: Session, attempt_id: str, *, gateway: CoverGateway,
            input_directory: Path, output_directory: Path, allow_submit: bool = True) -> str:
    """Perform bounded network work; paused rounds pass allow_submit=False.

    prepared -> uploading -> ready -> submitting -> submitted/queued/running
    -> succeeded (only after local durable download). Upload has no generation
    side effect; interrupted uploads can be explicitly recovered to prepared.
    A worker crash in submitting is ALWAYS query-only on the next invocation.
    """
    row = db.get(CoverGenerationAttempt, attempt_id)
    if row is None:
        raise CoverGatewayError("attempt_not_found")
    snapshot = json.loads(row.input_json)
    state = row.state
    if row.retry_after and row.retry_after > _now():
        return state
    if state in {"succeeded", "failed", "needs_review", "uploading", "downloading"}:
        return state
    if state == "download_pending":
        return _download(db, row, gateway, output_directory)
    if state in {"prepared", "ready"} and not allow_submit:
        return state
    if state == "prepared":
        if not _transition(db, attempt_id, "prepared", "uploading"):
            return "contended"
        try:
            data = (input_directory / snapshot["image_sha256"]).read_bytes()
            if hashlib.sha256(data).hexdigest() != snapshot["image_sha256"]:
                raise CoverGatewayError("input_snapshot_corrupted")
            uploaded = gateway.upload(data)
        except (CoverGatewayError, OSError) as exc:
            if isinstance(exc, CoverGatewayError) and exc.retryable:
                return _network_error(db, row, "uploading", "prepared", exc)
            _transition(db, attempt_id, "uploading", "needs_review",
                        error_code=getattr(exc, "code", "input_unavailable"))
            return "needs_review"
        _transition(db, attempt_id, "uploading", "ready", uploaded_image=uploaded,
                    retry_count=0, retry_after=None, error_code=None)
        return "ready"
    if state == "ready":
        uploaded_image = row.uploaded_image
        if not _transition(db, attempt_id, "ready", "submitting", gateway_unsettled=True):
            return "contended"
        try:
            job = gateway.submit(request_id=attempt_id, image=uploaded_image,
                                 prompt=snapshot["prompt"], parameters=CoverParameters(**snapshot["parameters"]))
        except CoverGatewayError as exc:
            if exc.uncertain:
                _transition(db, attempt_id, "submitting", "submitting", error_code=exc.code,
                            retry_after=None)
                return "submitting"
            return _network_error(db, row, "submitting", "ready", exc, rejected_submission=True)
        target = "needs_review" if job.state in {"submission_unknown", "history_unavailable"} else "submitted"
        _transition(db, attempt_id, "submitting", target, prompt_id=job.prompt_id,
                    error_code=job.state if target == "needs_review" else None,
                    retry_count=0, retry_after=None)
        return target
    if state not in {"submitting", "submitted", "queued", "running"}:
        raise CoverGatewayError("invalid_attempt_state")
    try:
        job = gateway.query(attempt_id)
    except CoverGatewayError as exc:
        # Even a 404 cannot prove a timed-out submission did not execute.
        if exc.uncertain:
            _transition(db, attempt_id, state, "needs_review", error_code=exc.code)
            return "needs_review"
        return _network_error(db, row, state, state, exc)
    if job.state in {"submission_unknown", "history_unavailable"}:
        _transition(db, attempt_id, state, "needs_review", error_code=job.state)
        return "needs_review"
    if job.state == "succeeded":
        if not _transition(db, attempt_id, state, "download_pending", prompt_id=job.prompt_id,
                           gateway_unsettled=False):
            return "contended"
        return _download(db, row, gateway, output_directory)
    _transition(db, attempt_id, state, job.state, prompt_id=job.prompt_id,
                gateway_unsettled=job.state != "failed", retry_count=0, retry_after=None,
                error_code="generation_failed" if job.state == "failed" else None)
    return job.state


def recover_local_stage(db: Session, attempt_id: str) -> bool:
    """Call only after proving the owning worker stopped (no time-based stealing).

    Upload/download can be safely repeated; submitting must remain query-only.
    """
    row = db.get(CoverGenerationAttempt, attempt_id)
    if row and row.state in {"uploading", "downloading"}:
        return _transition(db, attempt_id, row.state, "prepared" if row.state == "uploading" else "download_pending")
    return False


def latest_success(db: Session, sample_key: str) -> CoverGenerationAttempt | None:
    return db.scalar(select(CoverGenerationAttempt).where(
        CoverGenerationAttempt.sample_key == sample_key, CoverGenerationAttempt.state == "succeeded"
    ).order_by(CoverGenerationAttempt.sequence_no.desc()).limit(1))

===== FILE backend/app/cover_gateway.py =====
"""Short-request adapter for the fixed LAN cover gateway (not ComfyUI).

The caller owns the durable attempt ledger: persist request_id + uploaded image
before submit. This adapter never retries a POST or generates a replacement ID.
No browser Origin, bearer credentials, proxy environment or redirects are used.
"""
from __future__ import annotations

from dataclasses import dataclass
import hashlib
import io
import math
import os
from pathlib import Path
import re
import tempfile
from typing import Any
from urllib.parse import urlsplit

import httpx
from PIL import Image


REQUEST_ID = re.compile(r"[A-Za-z0-9_-]{1,80}")
UPLOAD_NAME = re.compile(r"lan-[0-9a-f]{32}\.png")
STATES = frozenset({"submitted", "queued", "running", "succeeded", "failed",
                    "submission_unknown", "history_unavailable"})
MAX_IMAGE_BYTES = 20 * 1024 * 1024


class CoverGatewayError(ValueError):
    def __init__(self, code: str, *, uncertain: bool = False, retryable: bool = False):
        # Never include response bodies, prompts or local paths in exceptions.
        super().__init__(code)
        self.code = code
        self.uncertain = uncertain
        self.retryable = retryable


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class CoverParameters:
    width: int = 512
    height: int = 512
    steps: int = 20
    seed: int = 20261019
    cfg: float = 4.0

    def validate(self) -> None:
        if any(type(v) is not int for v in (self.width, self.height, self.steps, self.seed)):
            raise CoverGatewayError("invalid_integer_parameter")
        if any(v < 256 or v > 1024 or v % 64 for v in (self.width, self.height)):
            raise CoverGatewayError("invalid_dimensions")
        if not 1 <= self.steps <= 40 or not 0 <= self.seed < 2**63:
            raise CoverGatewayError("invalid_generation_parameter")
        if type(self.cfg) not in (int, float) or not math.isfinite(self.cfg) or not 1 <= self.cfg <= 8:
            raise CoverGatewayError("invalid_cfg")


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class CoverJob:
    request_id: str
    state: str
    prompt_id: str | None


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class StoredCover:
    path: Path
    sha256: str
    size_bytes: int
    width: int
    height: int


def _request_id(value: str) -> str:
    if not isinstance(value, str) or not REQUEST_ID.fullmatch(value):
        raise CoverGatewayError("invalid_request_id")
    return value


def _image_info(data: bytes, *, png_only: bool = False) -> tuple[int, int]:
    if not data or len(data) > MAX_IMAGE_BYTES:
        raise CoverGatewayError("invalid_image_size")
    try:
        with Image.open(io.BytesIO(data)) as image:
            if image.format not in ({"PNG"} if png_only else {"PNG", "JPEG", "WEBP"}):
                raise CoverGatewayError("invalid_image_format")
            if image.width * image.height > 25_000_000:
                raise CoverGatewayError("invalid_image_dimensions")
            dimensions = image.size
            image.verify()
            return dimensions
    except CoverGatewayError:
        raise
    except Exception as exc:
        raise CoverGatewayError("invalid_image") from exc


def store_content(data: bytes, destination: Path) -> None:
    """Publish validated content by hash, repairing a truncated previous file."""
    digest = hashlib.sha256(data).hexdigest()
    destination.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    if destination.is_file() and hashlib.sha256(destination.read_bytes()).hexdigest() == digest:
        return
    temporary = None
    try:
        with tempfile.NamedTemporaryFile(dir=destination.parent, delete=False, suffix=".part") as file:
            temporary = Path(file.name)
            file.write(data); file.flush(); os.fsync(file.fileno())
        temporary.replace(destination)
        if os.name != "nt":
            fd = os.open(destination.parent, os.O_RDONLY | os.O_DIRECTORY)
            try:
                os.fsync(fd)
            finally:
                os.close(fd)
    finally:
        if temporary:
            temporary.unlink(missing_ok=True)


class CoverGateway:
    def __init__(self, base_url: str, *, transport: httpx.BaseTransport | None = None):
        url = urlsplit(base_url)
        if (url.scheme not in {"http", "https"} or not url.hostname or url.username
                or url.password or url.query or url.fragment or url.path not in {"", "/"}):
            raise CoverGatewayError("invalid_gateway_url")
        # Server-owned configuration only, never a caller-supplied image URL.
        self._client = httpx.Client(base_url=base_url.rstrip("/"), transport=transport,
                                   trust_env=False, follow_redirects=False,
                                   timeout=httpx.Timeout(30, connect=5))

    def close(self) -> None:
        self._client.close()

    def __enter__(self) -> CoverGateway:
        return self

    def __exit__(self, *_: Any) -> None:
        self.close()

    def _json(self, method: str, path: str, *, submitting: bool = False, **kwargs: Any) -> dict:
        try:
            response = self._client.request(method, path, **kwargs)
        except httpx.HTTPError as exc:
            raise CoverGatewayError("submission_unknown" if submitting else "gateway_unreachable",
                                    uncertain=submitting, retryable=not submitting) from exc
        if response.status_code == 429:
            raise CoverGatewayError("queue_full", retryable=True)
        if response.status_code == 404:
            # A missing gateway record after submission is not permission to regenerate.
            raise CoverGatewayError("job_not_found", uncertain=path.startswith("/v1/jobs/"))
        if not 200 <= response.status_code < 300:
            uncertain = submitting and (response.status_code >= 500 or response.status_code == 409)
            raise CoverGatewayError("submission_unknown" if uncertain else f"gateway_http_{response.status_code}",
                                    uncertain=uncertain, retryable=not submitting and response.status_code >= 500)
        try:
            value = response.json()
            if not isinstance(value, dict):
                raise ValueError("not an object")
            return value
        except ValueError as exc:
            raise CoverGatewayError("gateway_invalid_response", uncertain=submitting) from exc

    def health(self) -> dict:
        return self._json("GET", "/health")

    def upload(self, data: bytes) -> str:
        _image_info(data)
        response = self._json("POST", "/v1/images",
                              files={"image": ("input", data, "application/octet-stream")})
        name = response.get("image")
        if not isinstance(name, str) or not UPLOAD_NAME.fullmatch(name):
            raise CoverGatewayError("gateway_invalid_upload_name")
        return name

    @staticmethod
    def _job(value: dict, request_id: str, *, submitting: bool) -> CoverJob:
        state, prompt_id = value.get("state"), value.get("prompt_id")
        if (value.get("request_id") != request_id or state not in STATES
                or (prompt_id is not None and (not isinstance(prompt_id, str) or not prompt_id))
                or (state not in {"submission_unknown", "history_unavailable"} and not prompt_id)):
            raise CoverGatewayError("gateway_invalid_job", uncertain=submitting)
        return CoverJob(request_id, state, prompt_id)

    def submit(self, *, request_id: str, image: str | None, prompt: str,
               parameters: CoverParameters) -> CoverJob:
        _request_id(request_id)
        parameters.validate()
        if image is None:
            # Never pretend a blank reference image is a verified text-to-image model.
            raise CoverGatewayError("text_to_image_not_supported")
        if not isinstance(image, str) or not UPLOAD_NAME.fullmatch(image):
            raise CoverGatewayError("invalid_uploaded_image")
        if not isinstance(prompt, str) or not prompt.strip() or len(prompt) > 8000:
            raise CoverGatewayError("invalid_prompt")
        body = {"request_id": request_id, "image": image, "prompt": prompt,
                "width": parameters.width, "height": parameters.height,
                "steps": parameters.steps, "seed": parameters.seed, "cfg": parameters.cfg}
        return self._job(self._json("POST", "/v1/jobs", submitting=True, json=body),
                         request_id, submitting=True)

    def query(self, request_id: str) -> CoverJob:
        _request_id(request_id)
        return self._job(self._json("GET", f"/v1/jobs/{request_id}"), request_id, submitting=False)

    def download(self, request_id: str, *, directory: Path) -> StoredCover:
        """Download to a content-addressed owned store; never overwrite another result."""
        _request_id(request_id)
        data = bytearray()
        try:
            with self._client.stream("GET", f"/v1/jobs/{request_id}/image") as response:
                if response.status_code != 200:
                    raise CoverGatewayError(f"download_http_{response.status_code}", retryable=True)
                for chunk in response.iter_bytes(64 * 1024):
                    data.extend(chunk)
                    if len(data) > MAX_IMAGE_BYTES:
                        raise CoverGatewayError("invalid_image_size")
        except httpx.HTTPError as exc:
            raise CoverGatewayError("download_interrupted", retryable=True) from exc
        width, height = _image_info(bytes(data), png_only=True)
        digest = hashlib.sha256(data).hexdigest()
        destination = directory / f"{digest}.png"
        store_content(bytes(data), destination)
        return StoredCover(destination, digest, len(data), width, height)

===== FILE backend/app/cover_regression.py =====
"""Image-generation stock rounds using existing baseline sets as sample sources."""
from __future__ import annotations

from datetime import datetime, timezone
import json
from pathlib import Path
import uuid

from sqlalchemy import ForeignKey, Integer, String, Text, UniqueConstraint, select, func, and_
from sqlalchemy.orm import Mapped, Session, mapped_column

from .database import Base, reserve_sqlite_immediate_transaction
from .cover_attempts import CoverGenerationAttempt, create_attempt, latest_success, can_retry, can_regenerate
from .cover_gateway import CoverGatewayError, CoverParameters, MAX_IMAGE_BYTES


class CoverRegressionRun(Base):
    __tablename__ = "cover_regression_runs"
    id: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(64), primary_key=True)
    baseline_set_id: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(ForeignKey("baseline_sets.id", ondelete="RESTRICT"))
    category_key: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(40), index=True)
    name: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(160))
    status: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(24), index=True)
    config_json: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(Text)
    created_by: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(80))
    created_at: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(40))
    last_dispatched: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(40), default="", server_default="")


class CoverRegressionItem(Base):
    __tablename__ = "cover_regression_items"
    __table_args__ = (UniqueConstraint("run_id", "baseline_item_id", name="uq_cover_run_baseline_item"),)
    id: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(64), primary_key=True)
    run_id: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(ForeignKey("cover_regression_runs.id", ondelete="RESTRICT"), index=True)
    baseline_item_id: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(ForeignKey("baseline_set_items.id", ondelete="RESTRICT"))
    asset_id: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(ForeignKey("assets.id", ondelete="RESTRICT"))
    original_name: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(500))
    input_snapshot_json: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(Text, default="{}", server_default="{}")
    input_error: Mapped[str | None] = mapped_column(String(80), nullable=True)


class CoverRegressionReview(Base):
    __tablename__ = "cover_regression_reviews"
    __table_args__ = (UniqueConstraint("item_id", "revision", name="uq_cover_review_revision"),)
    id: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(primary_key=True)
    item_id: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(ForeignKey("cover_regression_items.id", ondelete="RESTRICT"), index=True)
    attempt_id: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(ForeignKey("cover_generation_attempts.id", ondelete="RESTRICT"))
    revision: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(Integer)
    verdict: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(24))
    reason: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(Text)
    actor: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(80))
    created_at: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(40))


def create_round(db: Session, *, baseline_set_id: int, prompt: str, parameters: CoverParameters,
                 actor: str, settings, request_id: str) -> CoverRegressionRun:
    from .models import Asset, BaselineSet, BaselineSetItem
    from .cover_gateway import _request_id
    _request_id(request_id)
    parameters.validate()
    if not prompt.strip() or len(prompt) > 8000:
        raise CoverGatewayError("invalid_prompt")
    config = json.dumps({"prompt": prompt, "parameters": vars(parameters),
                         "mode": "image_edit", "provider": "cover-image-lan-v1"}, sort_keys=True)
    reserve_sqlite_immediate_transaction(db)
    baseline = db.scalar(select(BaselineSet).where(BaselineSet.id == baseline_set_id)
                         .with_for_update().execution_options(populate_existing=True))
    previous = db.get(CoverRegressionRun, request_id)
    if previous:
        if previous.baseline_set_id != baseline_set_id or previous.config_json != config:
            raise CoverGatewayError("request_conflict")
        return previous
    if baseline is None:
        raise CoverGatewayError("baseline_not_found")
    samples = db.execute(select(BaselineSetItem, Asset).outerjoin(Asset, Asset.id == BaselineSetItem.asset_id)
                         .where(BaselineSetItem.baseline_set_id == baseline_set_id)
                         .order_by(BaselineSetItem.id)).all()
    if not samples:
        raise CoverGatewayError("baseline_empty")
    run = CoverRegressionRun(id=request_id, baseline_set_id=baseline.id, category_key=baseline.category_key,
        name=baseline.name, status="running", config_json=config, created_by=actor,
        created_at=datetime.now(timezone.utc).isoformat())
    db.add(run)
    db.flush()
    for sample, asset in samples:
        if asset is None or asset.category_key != baseline.category_key:
            raise CoverGatewayError("sample_category_mismatch")
        item = CoverRegressionItem(id=uuid.uuid4().hex, run_id=run.id, baseline_item_id=sample.id,
            asset_id=asset.id, original_name=asset.original_name,
            input_snapshot_json=json.dumps({name:getattr(asset,name) for name in (
                "stored_name","storage_backend","source_uri","sha256","mime_type","size_bytes")},ensure_ascii=False))
        db.add(item)
    db.flush()
    if round_statistics(db, run.id)["execution_finished"]:
        run.status = "completed"
    return run


def prepare_input(db: Session, item_id: str, *, settings) -> CoverGenerationAttempt | None:
    """Copy one frozen content version in the background, never follow new bytes."""
    from types import SimpleNamespace
    import hashlib,re
    from .nas_storage import resolve_asset_path, NasStorageError
    reserve_sqlite_immediate_transaction(db)
    run_id=db.scalar(select(CoverRegressionItem.run_id).where(CoverRegressionItem.id==item_id))
    run=db.scalar(select(CoverRegressionRun).where(CoverRegressionRun.id==run_id)
                  .with_for_update().execution_options(populate_existing=True))
    if run is None or run.status!='running':return None
    item=db.scalar(select(CoverRegressionItem).where(CoverRegressionItem.id==item_id)
                   .with_for_update().execution_options(populate_existing=True))
    previous=db.scalar(select(CoverGenerationAttempt).where(CoverGenerationAttempt.sample_key==item_id).limit(1))
    if previous or item.input_error:return previous
    try:
        frozen=json.loads(item.input_snapshot_json)
        if not re.fullmatch(r'[0-9a-f]{64}',frozen.get('sha256','')):
            raise CoverGatewayError('input_checksum_missing')
        source=resolve_asset_path(SimpleNamespace(**frozen),settings)
        with source.open('rb') as file:image=file.read(MAX_IMAGE_BYTES+1)
        if len(image)>MAX_IMAGE_BYTES:raise CoverGatewayError('invalid_image_size')
        if hashlib.sha256(image).hexdigest()!=frozen['sha256']:
            raise CoverGatewayError('input_snapshot_changed')
        config=json.loads(run.config_json)
        attempt=create_attempt(db,sample_key=item.id,sequence_no=1,image=image,prompt=config['prompt'],
            parameters=CoverParameters(**config['parameters']),input_directory=settings.data_dir/'stock-images'/'inputs')
        run.last_dispatched=datetime.now(timezone.utc).isoformat()
        return attempt
    except (CoverGatewayError,NasStorageError,OSError,ValueError) as exc:
        item.input_error=getattr(exc,'code','input_unavailable')
        run.last_dispatched=datetime.now(timezone.utc).isoformat()
        db.flush()
        return None


def retry_input(db: Session, item_id: str) -> None:
    reserve_sqlite_immediate_transaction(db)
    run_id=db.scalar(select(CoverRegressionItem.run_id).where(CoverRegressionItem.id==item_id))
    run=db.scalar(select(CoverRegressionRun).where(CoverRegressionRun.id==run_id)
                  .with_for_update().execution_options(populate_existing=True))
    if run is None or run.status=='cancelled':raise CoverGatewayError('round_cancelled')
    item=db.scalar(select(CoverRegressionItem).where(CoverRegressionItem.id==item_id)
                   .with_for_update().execution_options(populate_existing=True))
    if not item.input_error or db.scalar(select(CoverGenerationAttempt.id).where(CoverGenerationAttempt.sample_key==item_id).limit(1)):
        raise CoverGatewayError('input_not_retryable')
    item.input_error=None
    if run.status=='completed':run.status='running'
    db.flush()


def change_status(db: Session, run_id: str, action: str) -> CoverRegressionRun:
    reserve_sqlite_immediate_transaction(db)
    run = db.scalar(select(CoverRegressionRun).where(CoverRegressionRun.id == run_id).with_for_update())
    if run is None:
        raise CoverGatewayError("round_not_found")
    if action == "pause" and run.status in {"running", "paused"}:
        run.status = "paused"
    elif action == "resume" and run.status in {"paused", "running"}:
        run.status = "running"
    elif action == "cancel":
        run.status = "cancelled"
    else:
        raise CoverGatewayError("invalid_round_action")
    return run


def review_item(db: Session, *, item_id: str, attempt_id: str, expected_revision: int,
                verdict: str, reason: str, actor: str) -> CoverRegressionReview:
    if verdict not in {"adopt", "regenerate", "uncertain"}:
        raise CoverGatewayError("invalid_review_verdict")
    reserve_sqlite_immediate_transaction(db)
    item = db.scalar(select(CoverRegressionItem).where(CoverRegressionItem.id == item_id).with_for_update())
    if item is None:
        raise CoverGatewayError("item_not_found")
    attempt = db.get(CoverGenerationAttempt, attempt_id)
    if attempt is None or attempt.sample_key != item_id or attempt.state != "succeeded":
        raise CoverGatewayError("review_result_unavailable")
    old = db.scalar(select(CoverRegressionReview).where(CoverRegressionReview.item_id == item_id)
                    .order_by(CoverRegressionReview.revision.desc()).limit(1).with_for_update())
    if (old.revision if old else 0) != expected_revision:
        raise CoverGatewayError("review_revision_conflict")
    row = CoverRegressionReview(item_id=item_id, attempt_id=attempt_id,
        revision=expected_revision + 1, verdict=verdict, reason=reason, actor=actor,
        created_at=datetime.now(timezone.utc).isoformat())
    db.add(row)
    db.flush()
    return row


def regenerate_item(db: Session, *, item_id: str, input_directory: Path) -> CoverGenerationAttempt:
    """Regeneration is explicit; ambiguous old attempts must first be reconciled."""
    reserve_sqlite_immediate_transaction(db)
    item = db.scalar(select(CoverRegressionItem).where(CoverRegressionItem.id == item_id).with_for_update())
    if item is None:
        raise CoverGatewayError("item_not_found")
    run = db.get(CoverRegressionRun, item.run_id)
    if run.status == "cancelled":
        raise CoverGatewayError("round_cancelled")
    last = db.scalar(select(CoverGenerationAttempt).where(CoverGenerationAttempt.sample_key == item_id)
                     .order_by(CoverGenerationAttempt.sequence_no.desc()).limit(1).with_for_update())
    if last is None:
        raise CoverGatewayError("input_snapshot_missing")
    if not can_regenerate(last):
        raise CoverGatewayError("original_attempt_not_reconciled")
    frozen = json.loads(last.input_json)
    source = input_directory / frozen["image_sha256"]
    with source.open("rb") as file:
        image = file.read(MAX_IMAGE_BYTES + 1)
    import hashlib
    if hashlib.sha256(image).hexdigest() != frozen["image_sha256"]:
        raise CoverGatewayError("input_snapshot_corrupted")
    if run.status == "completed":
        run.status = "running"
    return create_attempt(db, sample_key=item_id, sequence_no=last.sequence_no + 1,
        image=image, prompt=frozen["prompt"], parameters=CoverParameters(**frozen["parameters"]),
        input_directory=input_directory)


def round_statistics(db: Session, run_id: str) -> dict:
    """Count latest attempts across all pages, independently of manual review."""
    latest = (select(CoverGenerationAttempt.sample_key,
                     func.max(CoverGenerationAttempt.sequence_no).label("sequence"))
              .join(CoverRegressionItem, CoverRegressionItem.id == CoverGenerationAttempt.sample_key)
              .where(CoverRegressionItem.run_id == run_id)
              .group_by(CoverGenerationAttempt.sample_key).subquery())
    counts = dict(db.execute(select(CoverGenerationAttempt.state, func.count())
        .join(latest, and_(CoverGenerationAttempt.sample_key == latest.c.sample_key,
                          CoverGenerationAttempt.sequence_no == latest.c.sequence))
        .group_by(CoverGenerationAttempt.state)).all())
    total = db.scalar(select(func.count()).select_from(CoverRegressionItem).where(CoverRegressionItem.run_id == run_id))
    reviewed = db.scalar(select(func.count(func.distinct(CoverRegressionReview.item_id)))
        .join(CoverRegressionItem, CoverRegressionReview.item_id == CoverRegressionItem.id)
        .where(CoverRegressionItem.run_id == run_id))
    missing = db.scalar(select(func.count()).select_from(CoverRegressionItem)
        .where(CoverRegressionItem.run_id==run_id,CoverRegressionItem.input_error.is_not(None),
            ~select(CoverGenerationAttempt.id).where(CoverGenerationAttempt.sample_key==CoverRegressionItem.id).exists()))
    succeeded = counts.get("succeeded", 0)
    failed = counts.get("failed", 0) + missing
    needs_review = counts.get("needs_review", 0)
    pending = total - succeeded - failed - needs_review
    unresolved = db.scalar(select(func.count()).select_from(CoverGenerationAttempt)
        .join(latest, and_(CoverGenerationAttempt.sample_key == latest.c.sample_key,
                          CoverGenerationAttempt.sequence_no == latest.c.sequence))
        .where(CoverGenerationAttempt.state == "needs_review", CoverGenerationAttempt.gateway_unsettled.is_(True)))
    attempt_query=select(CoverGenerationAttempt).join(CoverRegressionItem,CoverGenerationAttempt.sample_key==CoverRegressionItem.id).where(CoverRegressionItem.run_id==run_id)
    all_attempts=attempt_query.subquery()
    attempt_total=db.scalar(select(func.count()).select_from(all_attempts))
    confirmed_jobs=db.scalar(select(func.count()).select_from(all_attempts).where(all_attempts.c.prompt_id.is_not(None)))
    generated_images=db.scalar(select(func.count()).select_from(all_attempts).where(all_attempts.c.state=='succeeded'))
    return {"total": total, "succeeded": succeeded, "failed": failed,
            "needs_review": needs_review, "pending": pending, "reviewed": reviewed,
            "generation_attempts":attempt_total,"confirmed_jobs":confirmed_jobs,"generated_images":generated_images,
            "execution_finished": pending == 0 and unresolved == 0, "results_complete": succeeded == total and total > 0}


def settle_rounds(db: Session) -> None:
    for run in db.scalars(select(CoverRegressionRun).where(CoverRegressionRun.status == "running")
                          .with_for_update()):
        if round_statistics(db, run.id)["execution_finished"]:
            run.status = "completed"


def item_payload(db: Session, item: CoverRegressionItem) -> dict:
    attempts = db.scalars(select(CoverGenerationAttempt).where(CoverGenerationAttempt.sample_key == item.id)
                          .order_by(CoverGenerationAttempt.sequence_no.desc())).all()
    review = db.scalar(select(CoverRegressionReview).where(CoverRegressionReview.item_id == item.id)
                       .order_by(CoverRegressionReview.revision.desc()).limit(1))
    successful = next((a for a in attempts if a.state == "succeeded"), None)
    # A confirmed human selection remains authoritative after later AI generation.
    effective = next((a for a in attempts if review and a.id == review.attempt_id), successful)
    def elapsed(a):
        end=datetime.fromisoformat(a.updated_at) if a.state in {'succeeded','failed','needs_review'} else datetime.now(timezone.utc)
        return max(0,int((end-datetime.fromisoformat(a.created_at)).total_seconds()))
    return {"id": item.id, "asset_id": item.asset_id, "name": item.original_name,
        "input_error": item.input_error, "state": attempts[0].state if attempts else ("input_error" if item.input_error else "freezing"),
        "can_retry_input": not attempts and bool(item.input_error),
        "effective_attempt_id": effective.id if effective else None,
        "candidate_attempt_id": successful.id if successful and review and successful.id != review.attempt_id else None,
        "review": {"revision": review.revision, "verdict": review.verdict,
                   "reason": review.reason, "attempt_id": review.attempt_id} if review else None,
        "attempts": [{"id": a.id, "sequence": a.sequence_no, "state": a.state,
                      "error": a.error_code, "retry_count": a.retry_count,
                      "can_retry": can_retry(a),
                      "can_regenerate": can_regenerate(a), "updated_at":a.updated_at,
                      "elapsed_seconds":elapsed(a),
                      "can_resolve": a.state == "needs_review" and a.gateway_unsettled,
                      "resolution": json.loads(a.resolution_json) if a.resolution_json else None,
                      "can_recheck": a.state == "needs_review" and bool(a.uploaded_image) and
                          (a.gateway_unsettled or (a.error_code or "").startswith(("download_", "stored_image_"))),
                      "result": json.loads(a.result_json) if a.result_json else None}
                     for a in attempts]}

===== FILE backend/app/cover_regression_api.py =====
from __future__ import annotations

import json
import os
from typing import Callable, Literal

from fastapi import APIRouter, Depends, HTTPException, Query
from fastapi.responses import FileResponse
from pydantic import BaseModel, ConfigDict, Field
from sqlalchemy import select, func
from sqlalchemy.orm import Session
from sqlalchemy.exc import IntegrityError

from .authz import has_permission, require_categories
from .config import get_settings
from .database import get_db
from .cover_attempts import CoverGenerationAttempt, request_recheck, request_retry, resolve_unknown
from .cover_gateway import CoverGatewayError, CoverParameters
from .cover_regression import (CoverRegressionRun, CoverRegressionItem, change_status,
                               create_round, item_payload, regenerate_item, review_item, round_statistics, retry_input)


class CreateCoverRound(BaseModel):
    model_config = ConfigDict(extra="forbid")
    request_id: str = Field(min_length=1, max_length=64, pattern=r"^[A-Za-z0-9_-]+$")
    prompt: str = Field(min_length=1, max_length=8000)
    width: int = 512
    height: int = 512
    steps: int = Field(default=20, ge=1, le=40)
    seed: int = Field(default=20261019, ge=0, lt=2**63)
    cfg: float = Field(default=4.0, ge=1, le=8)


class CoverReviewRequest(BaseModel):
    model_config = ConfigDict(extra="forbid")
    attempt_id: str
    expected_revision: int = Field(ge=0)
    verdict: Literal["adopt", "regenerate", "uncertain"]
    reason: str = Field(default="", max_length=4000)


class CoverResolveRequest(BaseModel):
    model_config = ConfigDict(extra="forbid")
    evidence: str = Field(min_length=1, max_length=2000)
    expected_updated_at: str = Field(min_length=1, max_length=40)
    confirmed: Literal[True]


def build_cover_regression_router(current_user: Callable) -> APIRouter:
    router = APIRouter(tags=["stock-image-generation"])

    def writable(user, category):
        if not has_permission(user, "reviews:write"):
            raise HTTPException(403, "需要该业务的纠偏权限")
        require_categories(user, {category})

    def run_row(db, run_id):
        row = db.get(CoverRegressionRun, run_id)
        if row is None:
            raise HTTPException(404, "生图回归不存在")
        return row

    def item_row(db, run_id, item_id):
        item = db.get(CoverRegressionItem, item_id)
        if item is None or item.run_id != run_id:
            raise HTTPException(404, "样本不属于本轮")
        return item

    def summary(row):
        return {"id": row.id, "baseline_set_id": row.baseline_set_id, "name": row.name,
                "category_key": row.category_key, "status": row.status,
                "config": json.loads(row.config_json), "created_at": row.created_at}

    @router.get("/api/cover-regressions/capabilities")
    def capabilities(user=Depends(current_user)):
        return {"image_edit": bool(os.environ.get("COVER_GATEWAY_URL")), "text_to_image": False,
                "validated_sizes": [512], "human_review_required": False,
                "downstream_write": False, "estimated_seconds_per_image": 490,
                "cost": None}

    @router.post("/api/baseline-sets/{baseline_set_id}/cover-regressions")
    def create(baseline_set_id: int, payload: CreateCoverRound, user=Depends(current_user), db: Session = Depends(get_db)):
        from .models import BaselineSet
        baseline = db.get(BaselineSet, baseline_set_id)
        if baseline is None:
            raise HTTPException(404, "基准集不存在")
        writable(user, baseline.category_key)
        if not os.environ.get("COVER_GATEWAY_URL"):
            raise HTTPException(503, "尚未配置生图网关")
        try:
            row = create_round(db, baseline_set_id=baseline_set_id, prompt=payload.prompt,
                parameters=CoverParameters(**payload.model_dump(exclude={"request_id", "prompt"})),
                actor=user.username, settings=get_settings(), request_id=payload.request_id)
            db.commit()
            return summary(row)
        except CoverGatewayError as exc:
            db.rollback()
            raise HTTPException(409, exc.code) from exc
        except IntegrityError as exc:
            db.rollback()
            # A cross-baseline reuse of the same request ID must not return 500.
            raise HTTPException(409, "request_conflict") from exc

    @router.get("/api/cover-regressions")
    def listing(baseline_set_id: int | None = None, user=Depends(current_user), db: Session = Depends(get_db)):
        query = select(CoverRegressionRun)
        if baseline_set_id is not None:
            query = query.where(CoverRegressionRun.baseline_set_id == baseline_set_id)
        return {"items": [summary(row) for row in db.scalars(query.order_by(CoverRegressionRun.created_at.desc()).limit(100))]}

    @router.get("/api/cover-regressions/{run_id}")
    def detail(run_id: str, offset: int = Query(0, ge=0), limit: int = Query(50, ge=1, le=100),
               user=Depends(current_user), db: Session = Depends(get_db)):
        row = run_row(db, run_id)
        total = db.scalar(select(func.count()).select_from(CoverRegressionItem).where(CoverRegressionItem.run_id == run_id))
        items = db.scalars(select(CoverRegressionItem).where(CoverRegressionItem.run_id == run_id)
                          .order_by(CoverRegressionItem.baseline_item_id).offset(offset).limit(limit)).all()
        return {**summary(row), "total": total, "offset": offset,
                "statistics": round_statistics(db, row.id), "items": [item_payload(db, i) for i in items]}

    @router.post("/api/cover-regressions/{run_id}/items/{item_id}/attempts/{attempt_id}/recheck")
    def recheck(run_id: str, item_id: str, attempt_id: str,
                user=Depends(current_user), db: Session = Depends(get_db)):
        run = run_row(db, run_id)
        writable(user, run.category_key)
        item = item_row(db, run_id, item_id)
        attempt = db.get(CoverGenerationAttempt, attempt_id)
        if not attempt or attempt.sample_key != item.id:
            raise HTTPException(404, "生成任务不属于此素材")
        try:
            request_recheck(db, attempt_id)
            if run.status == "completed":
                run.status = "running"
            db.commit()
            return item_payload(db, item)
        except CoverGatewayError as exc:
            db.rollback()
            raise HTTPException(409, exc.code) from exc

    @router.post("/api/cover-regressions/{run_id}/items/{item_id}/attempts/{attempt_id}/retry")
    def retry(run_id: str, item_id: str, attempt_id: str,
              user=Depends(current_user), db: Session = Depends(get_db)):
        run = run_row(db, run_id)
        writable(user, run.category_key)
        item = item_row(db, run_id, item_id)
        attempt = db.get(CoverGenerationAttempt, attempt_id)
        if not attempt or attempt.sample_key != item.id:
            raise HTTPException(404, "生成任务不属于此素材")
        if run.status == "cancelled":
            raise HTTPException(409, "本轮已取消")
        try:
            request_retry(db, attempt_id)
            if run.status == "completed":
                run.status = "running"
            db.commit()
            return item_payload(db, item)
        except CoverGatewayError as exc:
            db.rollback()
            raise HTTPException(409, exc.code) from exc

    @router.post("/api/cover-regressions/{run_id}/items/{item_id}/attempts/{attempt_id}/resolve")
    def resolve(run_id: str, item_id: str, attempt_id: str, payload: CoverResolveRequest,
                user=Depends(current_user), db: Session = Depends(get_db)):
        writable(user, run_row(db, run_id).category_key)
        item = item_row(db, run_id, item_id)
        attempt = db.get(CoverGenerationAttempt, attempt_id)
        if not attempt or attempt.sample_key != item.id:
            raise HTTPException(404, "生成任务不属于此素材")
        try:
            resolve_unknown(db, attempt_id, actor=user.username, evidence=payload.evidence,
                            expected_updated_at=payload.expected_updated_at)
            db.commit()
            return item_payload(db, item)
        except CoverGatewayError as exc:
            db.rollback()
            raise HTTPException(409, exc.code) from exc

    @router.post("/api/cover-regressions/{run_id}/control/{action}")
    def control(run_id: str, action: Literal["pause", "resume", "cancel"],
                user=Depends(current_user), db: Session = Depends(get_db)):
        writable(user, run_row(db, run_id).category_key)
        try:
            row = change_status(db, run_id, action)
            db.commit()
            return summary(row)
        except CoverGatewayError as exc:
            raise HTTPException(409, exc.code) from exc

    @router.post("/api/cover-regressions/{run_id}/items/{item_id}/review")
    def review(run_id: str, item_id: str, payload: CoverReviewRequest,
               user=Depends(current_user), db: Session = Depends(get_db)):
        writable(user, run_row(db, run_id).category_key)
        item = item_row(db, run_id, item_id)
        try:
            review_item(db, item_id=item_id, actor=user.username, **payload.model_dump())
            db.commit()
            return item_payload(db, item)
        except CoverGatewayError as exc:
            raise HTTPException(409, exc.code) from exc

    @router.post("/api/cover-regressions/{run_id}/items/{item_id}/regenerate")
    def regenerate(run_id: str, item_id: str, user=Depends(current_user), db: Session = Depends(get_db)):
        writable(user, run_row(db, run_id).category_key)
        item = item_row(db, run_id, item_id)
        try:
            regenerate_item(db, item_id=item_id, input_directory=get_settings().data_dir / "stock-images" / "inputs")
            db.commit()
            return item_payload(db, item)
        except (CoverGatewayError, OSError) as exc:
            raise HTTPException(409, getattr(exc, "code", "input_snapshot_unavailable")) from exc

    @router.post("/api/cover-regressions/{run_id}/items/{item_id}/retry-input")
    def retry_source(run_id: str, item_id: str, user=Depends(current_user), db: Session = Depends(get_db)):
        writable(user, run_row(db, run_id).category_key)
        item=item_row(db,run_id,item_id)
        try:
            retry_input(db,item.id);db.commit()
            return item_payload(db,item)
        except CoverGatewayError as exc:
            db.rollback()
            raise HTTPException(409,exc.code) from exc

    @router.get("/api/cover-regressions/{run_id}/items/{item_id}/attempts/{attempt_id}/image")
    def image(run_id: str, item_id: str, attempt_id: str,
              user=Depends(current_user), db: Session = Depends(get_db)):
        item_row(db, run_id, item_id)
        attempt = db.get(CoverGenerationAttempt, attempt_id)
        if attempt is None or attempt.sample_key != item_id or attempt.state != "succeeded":
            raise HTTPException(404, "图片尚未生成")
        result = json.loads(attempt.result_json)
        import re
        digest = result.get("sha256", "")
        if not re.fullmatch(r"[0-9a-f]{64}", digest):
            raise HTTPException(409, "图片存储记录异常")
        path = get_settings().data_dir / "stock-images" / "outputs" / f"{digest}.png"
        if not path.is_file():
            raise HTTPException(404, "图片文件不可用")
        return FileResponse(path, media_type="image/png", headers={"Cache-Control": "private, max-age=3600"})

    return router

===== FILE backend/app/cover_worker.py =====
"""Opt-in short-tick image worker. No in-memory generation queue.

The global advisory lock serializes short HTTP interactions, not GPU execution.
The gateway retains at most two submitted jobs; remaining work stays in SQL.
"""
from contextlib import contextmanager
from datetime import datetime, timezone, timedelta
import os
from pathlib import Path

from sqlalchemy import select, text, or_, and_
from sqlalchemy.orm import Session
from sqlalchemy.pool import NullPool
from sqlalchemy import create_engine

from .cover_attempts import CoverGenerationAttempt, advance, recover_local_stage
from .cover_gateway import CoverGateway
from .cover_regression import CoverRegressionItem, CoverRegressionRun, settle_rounds, prepare_input
from .database import reserve_sqlite_immediate_transaction


@contextmanager
def scheduler_lock(engine, directory: Path):
    """Never steal a live worker's lock. Connection/process death releases it."""
    if engine.dialect.name == "mysql":
        lock_engine = create_engine(engine.url, poolclass=NullPool)
        with lock_engine.connect() as connection:
            acquired = connection.execute(text("SELECT GET_LOCK('stock-cover-worker', 0)")).scalar() == 1
            try:
                yield acquired
            finally:
                if acquired:
                    connection.execute(text("SELECT RELEASE_LOCK('stock-cover-worker')"))
        lock_engine.dispose()
        return
    if engine.dialect.name != "sqlite":
        raise RuntimeError("cover worker requires verified SQLite or MySQL locking")
    directory.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    with (directory / "worker.lock").open("a+b") as file:
        file.seek(0, 2)
        if file.tell() == 0:
            file.write(b"0"); file.flush()
        file.seek(0)
        acquired = False
        try:
            if os.name == "nt":
                import msvcrt
                msvcrt.locking(file.fileno(), msvcrt.LK_NBLCK, 1)
            else:
                import fcntl
                fcntl.flock(file.fileno(), fcntl.LOCK_EX | fcntl.LOCK_NB)
            acquired = True
        except OSError:
            pass
        try:
            yield acquired
        finally:
            if acquired:
                file.seek(0)
                if os.name == "nt":
                    msvcrt.locking(file.fileno(), msvcrt.LK_UNLCK, 1)
                else:
                    fcntl.flock(file.fileno(), fcntl.LOCK_UN)


def tick(engine, *, directory: Path, gateway: CoverGateway, settings=None) -> bool:
    with scheduler_lock(engine, directory) as acquired:
        if not acquired:
            return False
        with Session(engine) as db:
            settle_rounds(db)
            db.commit()
            # Holding the global worker lock proves no older worker is uploading
            # or downloading. It does NOT prove a submitted GPU job has stopped.
            recovering = db.scalars(select(CoverGenerationAttempt).where(
                CoverGenerationAttempt.state.in_(["uploading", "downloading"]))).all()
            for row in recovering:
                recover_local_stage(db, row.id)
            threshold = (datetime.now(timezone.utc) - timedelta(seconds=5)).isoformat()
            pending = db.scalar(select(CoverGenerationAttempt).where(
                CoverGenerationAttempt.state.in_(["submitting", "submitted", "queued", "running", "download_pending"]),
                CoverGenerationAttempt.updated_at <= threshold,
                or_(CoverGenerationAttempt.retry_after.is_(None), CoverGenerationAttempt.retry_after <= datetime.now(timezone.utc).isoformat())
            ).order_by(CoverGenerationAttempt.updated_at).limit(1))
            if pending:
                advance(db, pending.id, gateway=gateway, input_directory=directory / "inputs",
                        output_directory=directory / "outputs", allow_submit=False)
                db.commit()
            # Snapshot bytes in the background even while the GPU is occupied,
            # so a 400-image launch never holds an HTTP request or a long batch
            # write transaction. One content hash/version is checked per tick.
            preparation=db.scalar(select(CoverRegressionItem.id).join(CoverRegressionRun)
                .where(CoverRegressionRun.status=='running',CoverRegressionItem.input_error.is_(None),
                    ~select(CoverGenerationAttempt.id).where(CoverGenerationAttempt.sample_key==CoverRegressionItem.id).exists())
                .order_by(CoverRegressionRun.last_dispatched,CoverRegressionRun.id,CoverRegressionItem.baseline_item_id).limit(1))
            if preparation:
                if settings is None:
                    from .config import get_settings
                    settings=get_settings()
                db.rollback()
                prepare_input(db,preparation,settings=settings)
                db.commit()
            # Unknown jobs reserve capacity until reconciled; don't fill the
            # gateway with replacements while old tasks may still be running.
            in_flight = db.scalars(select(CoverGenerationAttempt.id).where(
                or_(CoverGenerationAttempt.state.in_(["submitting", "submitted", "queued", "running"]),
                    and_(CoverGenerationAttempt.state == "needs_review",
                         CoverGenerationAttempt.gateway_unsettled.is_(True)))
            )).all()
            if len(in_flight) >= 2:
                return pending is not None or preparation is not None
            candidate = db.execute(select(CoverRegressionRun.id, CoverGenerationAttempt.id)
                .join(CoverRegressionItem, CoverRegressionItem.run_id == CoverRegressionRun.id)
                .join(CoverGenerationAttempt, CoverGenerationAttempt.sample_key == CoverRegressionItem.id)
                .where(CoverRegressionRun.status == "running", CoverGenerationAttempt.state.in_(["prepared", "ready"]),
                       or_(CoverGenerationAttempt.retry_after.is_(None), CoverGenerationAttempt.retry_after <= datetime.now(timezone.utc).isoformat()))
                .order_by(CoverRegressionRun.last_dispatched, CoverRegressionRun.id,
                          CoverGenerationAttempt.created_at).limit(1)).first()
            if not candidate:
                return pending is not None or preparation is not None
            run_id, attempt_id = candidate
            db.rollback()  # lock using a current read, not an earlier MySQL snapshot
            reserve_sqlite_immediate_transaction(db)
            run = db.scalar(select(CoverRegressionRun).where(CoverRegressionRun.id == run_id)
                            .with_for_update().execution_options(populate_existing=True))
            if run.status != "running":
                return False
            run.last_dispatched = datetime.now(timezone.utc).isoformat()
            db.flush()
            advance(db, attempt_id, gateway=gateway, input_directory=directory / "inputs",
                    output_directory=directory / "outputs", allow_submit=True)
            db.commit()
            return True


def process_cover_tick() -> bool:
    url = os.environ.get("COVER_GATEWAY_URL", "").strip()
    if not url:  # no production behavior change until explicitly configured
        return False
    from .config import get_settings
    from .database import engine
    with CoverGateway(url) as gateway:
        return tick(engine, directory=get_settings().data_dir / "stock-images", gateway=gateway, settings=get_settings())


if __name__ == "__main__":
    import logging
    import time
    if not os.environ.get("COVER_GATEWAY_URL"):
        raise SystemExit("COVER_GATEWAY_URL must be configured before starting this worker")
    while True:
        try:
            process_cover_tick()
        except Exception:
            logging.exception("生图任务处理失败，保留账本并等待下一轮")
        time.sleep(2)
